# Recoil Settings | PRAS Documentation

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings.md)
.

All settings are located in the **Recoil Data** asset (to create one, right-click and go **Miscellaneous -> Data Asset -> Recoil Data**). This asset contains many sections, and we will cover them one by one.

Controller Recoil[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#controller-recoil)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FtGQzxOdIBDRAdYq8pQoS%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc7b046fd-95b0-4631-abad-a625cbfb07a5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=08decf2b2b1c64f61f9561cd6dc40f8b&sv=3)

Controller Recoil.

*   **Horizontal Recoil Step**: a value will be computed in this range and added to the controller Yaw.
    
*   **Vertical Recoil Step**: a value will be computed in this range and added to the controller Pitch.
    
*   **Recoil Smoothing**: interpolation speed for X (Yaw) and Y (Pitch).
    
*   **Damping**: how fast the both Pitch and Yaw will go to zero,
    
*   **Compensation**: how much player input to take into account. 1 - 100%, 0 - 0%.
    

**Controller Recoil** is added directly to the **Player Controller** using _AddPitchInput_ and _AddYawInput_ methods.

Curve[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#curve)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section contains curves that we covered in the previous chapter:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fuf3hCnpZyMVd7Gru8iuJ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da0bf6c2d-e352-43b1-a343-f447802bd471&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=620c9871d93f733f5c8cad9862e003c3&sv=3)

Vector curves for translation and rotation,

Input[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#input)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

These properties generate main target recoil values:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FgywxkTJIaLKMl08mV8tW%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfb4e109a-53a2-4465-b70e-a706f073f68a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e31c788f57fc8f29992164964bae209d&sv=3)

Input properties.

*   **Pitch/Yaw/Roll Aim**: a value will be generated in this range and added to the pitch/yaw/roll when aiming.
    
*   **Pitch/Yaw/Roll**: a value will be generated in this range and added to the pitch/yaw/roll when hip firing.
    
*   **Kick/Kick R/ Kick Up Aim**: a value will be generated in this range and added in the forward/right/up direction when aiming.
    
*   **Kick/Kick R/ Kick Up**: a value will be generated in this range and added in the forward/right/up direction when aiming.
    

It's worth noting how the random generation happens in runtime:

*   For 2D vectors, it will compute a value via FRandRange(X, Y)
    
*   For 4D vectors, it will compute a value via FRandRange(FRandRange(X, Y), FRandRange(Z, W)). We do it this way to avoid getting close-to-zero values, which sometimes look horrible.
    

Smoothing[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#smoothing)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This group is only applied when auto/burst firing.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FsOG9EpgDj4bSl96QdvFv%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0c2cd237-3c00-466e-b57a-14dc4d25d248&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da93c3261258179ed5edb97a9e2df91b&sv=3)

Smoothing properties.

*   **Smooth Rot/Loc**: interpolation speed for a rotation/translation axis. If the value is zero - no interpolation will be applied.
    
*   **Multi Rot/Loc**: multipliers for a rotation/translation axis.
    
*   **Smooth Roll**: if roll sign should change every shot. Keep it true to achieve the best results.
    

Noise Layer[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#noise-layer)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This layer applies translation 2D noise in the YZ plane (right and up movement).

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252F50rKI89ROjnWH6tb1CgQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc24a7703-5104-4920-b427-d7d810ed8f18&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0a6f39299c2ff8f0507acce8e1264c81&sv=3)

Noise properties.

*   **Noise 2DTarget**: this value will be added to the translation of every shot.
    
*   **Noise 2DAccel**: interpolation speed for right and up axes.
    
*   **Noise 2DZero**: how fast each axis will go to zero.
    
*   **Noise Aim Scalar**: multiplier applied when aiming.
    

Pushback[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#pushback)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This layer is applied on the second shot of a burst or auto sequence.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FI8PKIOllkjMcm0XKBDlS%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4cc65807-9290-41ec-9bc2-21d4f73254aa&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7f27c96c816c7d8ef2c4f1b91e41b213&sv=3)

Pushback properties.

*   **Pushback**: this value will be added to the forward translation.
    
*   **Pushback Zero**: how fast the pushback should go to zero.
    
*   **Pushback Accel**: pusback interpolation speed.
    

Recoil Sway[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#recoil-sway)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This layer applies sway to simulate a body recoil.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FCz7TjkOlPPylFGnIIRPp%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D085af73f-496f-4f67-9522-f8d2f013557a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=968e678530ab9b22d7c95a4e873a0738&sv=3)

Recoil Sway properties.

*   **Pitch Sway**: a value will be randomly generated in this range and added to the pitch.
    
*   **Yaw Sway**: a value will be randomly generated in this range and added to the yaw.
    
*   **Roll Sway Multiplier**: will multiply the yaw result by this value and add it to the roll.
    
*   **Damping**: how fast the sway should go to zero.
    
*   **Acceleration**: interpolation sway speed.
    
*   **Ads Scale**: this value will scale the recoil sway when aiming.
    
*   **Pivot Offset**: will offset the recoil physical pivot.
    

Timeline[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#timeline)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

These properties control the general animation timeline.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FY16d7loQdXh201o1zGIC%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da18dec5e-c78a-4531-8c24-f20d68bed40e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=785ab2d82c58c33b713d778b119eba43&sv=3)

Tineline properties.

*   **Playback Offset**: will offset the loop time interval. For example, if a weapon fire rate is 600RPM, the loop interval will be 0.1s + Playback Offset.
    
*   **Play Rate**: animation speed multiplier.
    

Pivot[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings#pivot)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section controls the physical recoil pivot point.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fw1anlwSIrkPt8CRk8wiQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2a12b9c3-7377-48ba-b288-1fda5afd4824&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9873e1be11c5a84592a575aae77dcb28&sv=3)

Pivot settings.

*   **Pivot Offset**: applied when hip firing.
    
*   **Ads Pivot Offset**: applied when aiming.
    

**Tip**: pivot offsets can be useful when we want to apply recoil rotation around a certain point, like a shoulder.

# Getting Set Up | PRAS Documentation

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/getting-set-up.md)
.

**Tip**: make sure to already implement basic firing logic in your project.

Step 1 - Recoil Animation Component[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/getting-set-up#step-1-recoil-animation-component)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

First, it is essential to add the **Recoil Animation Component** to the character.

Blueprints

C++

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252F4wyQRZRa2i08pclSWsQj%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da18eb5bc-9d03-4725-a1d1-6ed14f92dc5a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4c6f80366007eddd0252fe93d4a1d055&sv=3)

Copy

    UCLASS(config=Game)
    class AYourCharacterClass : public ACharacter
    {
        GENERATED_BODY()
    
    public:
        AYourCharacterClass();
    
    protected:
        UPROPERTY(EditDefaultsOnly, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Recoil")
        TObjectPtr<URecoilAnimationComponent> RecoilComponent;
    
        ...
    };

Then, in your character class click on the RecoilAnimation component and specify the Helper UI:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252F9Gmeeb2aeyrSNgY2rKZT%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D19855adf-c124-4b72-8197-61d089727171&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=35847383bc7ded05047e46d6e5c366ca&sv=3)

Select this blueprint.

The Helper UI is useful when you want to modify the recoil values in runtime in a very convenient way:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FLRJ92IQm7scBIPm2Ggci%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dca8d9f41-ad58-467a-aaf1-dfe2acf7f97e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3f520ba02da9f6d3a17fd3470885ac03&sv=3)

To enable this Helper UI in the game you can use the Blueprint-exposed methods:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FSKhTkMdSkG87OHj1IOBh%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df9e799a4-7c55-4a5c-a947-b6fe0c53a845&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3b549b165ade8ecb3c9c903fdc9cef41&sv=3)

How to use helper UI.

Step 2 - Initialize and Play[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/getting-set-up#step-2-initialize-and-play)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Make sure to call the `Init` method of the **Recoil Animation Component** to initialize the recoil animation:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FNGo3aI0bJbI5aiaxiC3O%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D57ca488f-80ac-4be5-84fc-38fe8c8a2d88&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7c9e8c45dbe71d7b1ca373e4fe24c03b&sv=3)

Init function.

*   **Data**: **Recoil Data** asset.
    
*   **Rate**: fire rate or RPM (Rounds-per-minute).
    
*   **Burst:** length of the burst sequence**.**
    

Next, call `Play` and `Stop` methods when firing:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fwwsbla8WWkwDMOerwant%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dde35e9d4-4b22-43d3-ad21-aecf5faefa61&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=528ede1aa2700312b8c58cebbfedac6d&sv=3)

Firing logic.

**Note**: Play must be called every shot!

Step 3 - Animation Blueprint Integration[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/getting-set-up#step-3-animation-blueprint-integration)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Make sure to add a **Recoil Animation** node to the Anim Graph:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Ft4NrSaox5umi2XKQr6uv%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9d748092-77ba-4b30-a325-dc644d87c46d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=dc3cda920b342d0b9850c1590ea77138&sv=3)

This node will apply recoil.

If you already have an _IK_ system set up, select the _IK_ or a _VB_ (virtual bone) you want to animate with recoil in the node settings:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FCWI5cErNp3NRPB4rNvaa%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dbdaa648d-13e4-4032-a727-4baf75290c20&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=944728471fc5a5a5334d4ba2c34188ea&sv=3)

Node settings.

### If you need help with IK[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/getting-set-up#if-you-need-help-with-ik)

First, add Virtual Bones to the character head:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FKir2cdAMDEQdfBhHoMeY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3d830ad4-5599-4786-910b-1e2e8f1f1389&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c4935b0dfc6000a69777917c0d2cd46&sv=3)

VB stands for Virtual Bone.

*   **VB WeaponPivot** targets ik\_hand\_gun.
    
*   **VB hand\_r** targets hand\_r.
    
*   **VB hand\_l** targets hand\_l.
    

Add **CopyBone** nodes before the **Recoil Animation** nodes:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fw1UK9d1se7me6b0kCKdH%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd184c38a-565f-4f8c-8c3c-92011158cdea&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=08dcaa63f03d4a84919dfa322b6c7763&sv=3)

These will copy animation from FK bones to VBs.

If your animations do not keyframe the **ik\_hand\_gun** bone (it is used for baking gun movement into the character animation), you will have to use **hand\_r** as a source bone in the first **CopyBone** node. You can additionally offset the **VB WeaponPivot** with a custom offset if you want more flexibility.

Finally, use **Two Bone IK** after the **Recoil Animation** node:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FAMxlxBIIVVW97C4GAtzV%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De38e011e-3271-4b84-a47b-f1e37908cdf9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5e776a4322b16d97b3d5ae38cf8f618c&sv=3)

IK pass.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FGo9YyPxponov5NI7JjCo%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddc7bee46-946a-4cc7-abe9-72bbf8c12fd7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1b3fb508d31f44445334d0c204c88124&sv=3)

Preferred IK settings.

* * *

At this point, the setup is complete. If recoil is still not playing, double-check the steps above or:

*   Check if **Recoil Data** has any values.
    
*   The correct bone is animated by the **RecoilAnimation**.
    
*   **Play** method is called every shot.
    
*   The **Fire Rate** is in rounds-per-minute and greater than zero.
    

If the problem persists, feel free to post a ticket on our [Discord](https://discord.gg/kinemation-1027338787958816860)
.

[PreviousRecoil Settings](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings)
[NextHow to implement attachments](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/how-to-implement-attachments)

# Recoil Animation | PRAS Documentation

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-animation.md)
.

How Does It Work?[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-animation#how-does-it-work)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The **PRAS (Procedural Recoil Animation System)** uses an advanced, yet simple way to generate realistic recoil. The system takes Vector Curves for translation and rotation, plays them in runtime, and uses curve values as interpolation alphas to reach the randomly generated targets.

> RecoilAnimation = Lerp(0f, TargetValue, CurveValue)

where **Target Value** is randomly generated when a shot is fired.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FbGyBFT8oA2snyTlu8Z2W%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc597fb21-26e5-4a41-a303-be413e5b20f8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ab012f8512cbba09cb6e79c6e08eded9&sv=3)

Example recoil curve.

Recoil curves should be in the range \[-1;1\], so a curve value can be used as an interpolation alpha. The curve peaks at 1 and then goes down to 0 with some oscillation.

**Tip**: because curves are much simpler in comparison to other methods (e.g. spring interpolation), the recoil animation is frame-rate independent.

Single and Auto Fire[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-animation#single-and-auto-fire)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**Recoil Data** is an asset, which contains all information about the recoil animation. This includes curves and other settings:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fuf3hCnpZyMVd7Gru8iuJ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da0bf6c2d-e352-43b1-a343-f447802bd471&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=620c9871d93f733f5c8cad9862e003c3&sv=3)

Curve section in the Recoil Data.

To create a new **Recoil Data**, right-click and go to **Miscellaneous -> Data Asset -> Recoil Data**. This asset includes 4 _Vector Curves_ in total: 2 for translation and 2 for rotation. The PRAS has 2 main fire modes:

*   Single
    
*   Auto/Burst (there's no technical difference animation-wise)
    

**Tip**: we use different curves for single and auto fire because of animation quality. If we use single-fire curves when auto-firing, the animation will look choppy. To make it look smoother and more fluid we use a different set of curves.

Let's compare an example single and auto fire curve:

Single

Auto/Burst

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fj9OuQEgdKvPOsUFSmIo0%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1956567a-ad4c-4338-9664-9a3ea89beab8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3f5992d7868c2638b97e73e9f6f9d0e2&sv=3)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FS656FcC1900ZLIaUsT2X%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D949f1a80-ef7b-4791-a2e3-5cbccb400760&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0dce328e36970fda7525342a21b5e7ee&sv=3)

The curves are almost identical! The only difference is that auto curve values are very close to 0 at some time - this time equals the fire rate of a weapon (in this case 600RPM = 0.1s). This will ensure that the weapon's main muzzle climbing will be fully preserved when auto-firing.

**Tip**: regardless of a fire mode, the first shot is **always** a single fire shot.

[PreviousWelcome!](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation)
[NextRecoil Settings](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/basics/recoil-settings)

Last updated 1 year ago

# How to implement attachments | PRAS Documentation

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/how-to-implement-attachments.md)
.

Overview[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/how-to-implement-attachments#overview)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To modify the current recoil animation, **PRAS** has the Input Scale feature using these methods:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FONnChPSmiaEpMSgWqprq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D015fe425-65cd-47bf-9d32-95ecd43da4ad&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6bbb14836f18c0112e2f868e5aa29fb5&sv=3)

Input Scale methods.

*   **Scale Input**: applies an Input Scale.
    
*   **Unscale Input**: reverts an Input Scale.
    
*   **Reset Input Scale**: resets any applied modifiers.
    

**Input Scale** is a struct that includes these properties:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fbe7QHNIcM9MlybwhiO1c%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1d6e8b3e-0656-430d-a4d8-347c0b8ddcfd&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3beee0d37e2f5f188d21ee68336490c4&sv=3)

Input Scale properties.

Use-case[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/tutorials/how-to-implement-attachments#use-case)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When attachment is equipped, make sure to update the current Input Scale:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252FnSh2cWXb85lRyJ24NYkN%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D485fd882-3569-44ff-92f1-f7d357622a4a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0a16645f47bab61f0b2f79ecc0beb8be&sv=3)

How to scale recoil.

To revert changes of the previous attachment:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/pras-documentation/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3651122009-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FmWlhCRI70uShLRiOey3T%252Fuploads%252Fnt7YFCLhUpqAPD7EtkJQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D06d3d633-5722-4016-af3a-698abfed3e96&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=70e24beda80ce4551c4d222a1eb0e827&sv=3)

How to revert recoil scale.

# Overview | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview.md)
.

Content[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#content)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

All content - animations, weapons, sounds, and Blueprints - is located in **KINEMATION/TacticalShooterPack**.:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F2CWswtKNyuoYsc2uUF8A%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deaf4fb15-99c7-4182-9b5c-a73b3fa46f01&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=97b57df659eb718e1ed59cd5b63f42af&sv=3)

Tactical Shooter Pack content.

The **Common** directory contains general Blueprints used across KINEMATION assets. Its contents are covered later in this section.

The Tactical Shooter Pack consists of **11** directories:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FvfWSqdQ3iwmsG9fKLeHo%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1bd9599f-f96a-4e95-8e14-67f64a7de0ec&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1ef12585adbeca410e2779b23b9de409&sv=3)

Content folders.

Let's break down each sub-folder to better explain what you can find there.

Animations[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#animations)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This folder contains camera, character, and weapon animations. It also includes Animation Blueprints for UE4 and UE5 skeletons.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FmcgIcrW0su4fxF2D7wYm%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da095252d-91a6-4968-af98-c24ded538cff&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9d426988f30369914219e22e477e8ea5&sv=3)

Animation Blueprints for UE5 Mannequin.

The main Animation Blueprint is **ABP\_TacticalShooter\_UE5**, which relies on these modules:

*   **ABP\_UE5\_Sway**: Applies procedural aiming and movement sway.
    
*   **ABP\_UE5\_IK\_Ads**: Applies procedural aiming.
    
*   **ABP\_UE5\_IK\_Main**: Applies general procedural animation (spine rotation, leaning, recoil, etc.).
    
*   **ABP\_UE5\_IK\_Pose**: Positions the weapon and hands via IK.
    

All Animation Blueprints depend on **AC\_TacticalShooterAnimation -** the central component in this asset. It supplies ABPs with key data (e.g., IK settings and firing state).

Blueprints[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#blueprints)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This folder contains all Blueprints used in the pack:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FsS0Q5RBvGtvTvwmyACKq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D72332f16-8379-4cbf-980a-61a2cebe3e6a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1c5c63a5c7fe5cfe3773649ca6ae06c6&sv=3)

Blueprint content.

*   **AdditiveMotions**: Procedural additive animations played when aiming or changing fire mode.
    
*   **Attachments**: Blueprint foundation for the attachment system _(work in progress)_.
    
*   **Data**: Settings and structs used in the pack (e.g., weapon animation settings).
    
*   **Notifies**: Custom Animation Notifies used in the pack.
    
*   **Recoil**: Recoil settings for each weapon.
    
*   **Weapons**: Game‑ready weapon Blueprints.
    

This folder also contains very important Blueprints, like:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fsv9PzxpVxIiG9amwirf4%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da017477f-9316-4d30-9912-6e8710260514&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5fd6bbb2b11670d4245048c017a5bc2d&sv=3)

Blueprint content.

*   **AC\_TacticalShooterAnimation:** Actor Component that connects gameplay and Animation Blueprints; treat it as an interface between the player and the animation system.
    
*   **BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter:** Game‑ready player Blueprint.
    
*   **BP\_TacticalShooterWeapon:** Base class for all weapons.
    
*   **BPI\_TacticalShooterCharacter:** Interface used by weapons and Animation Blueprints.
    
*   **BPI\_TacticalShooterWeapon:** Interface that passes data to the animation system.
    
*   **TacOperatorSettings** is an asset that contains meshes as well as weapons to use.
    

Camera, Casings, Character and Weapons[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#camera-casings-character-and-weapons)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section contains all skeletal and static meshes used in the package. The **Weapons** folder also includes attachments (e.g., magazines).

Demo[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#demo)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This directory includes a simple demo scene and the UE5 Manny.

Inputs[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#inputs)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This folder contains all **Input Actions** and the **Input Mapping Context** used by the player Blueprint.

SFX[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#sfx)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This folder contains all sound effects and sound Blueprints. Weapon‑specific sounds are divided into two categories: **(1)** Actions (e.g., reloads, inspects, mag checks) and **(2)** Firing.

UI[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#ui)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This folder contains a UMG widget used in the playable demo. It displays the control list and the currently equipped weapon.

VFX[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview#vfx)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This folder contains Data Assets for the Niagara casing system. Each asset is set up for a specific casing (e.g., NATO 5.56, 9 mm).

[PreviousInstallation](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/installation)
[NextGASP Integration](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration)

# Camera | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/camera.md)
.

How it works[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/camera#how-it-works)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The camera is animated using three curves:

*   **Camera\_Pitch**
    
*   **Camera\_Yaw**
    
*   **Camera\_Roll**
    

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F5tHHXA6Ql3IqjSzLgQIK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9850b0db-7a0b-454a-a98f-b08071d36d10&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=704d572c7a900fe6e6d2567e9057167b&sv=3)

Camera animation curves.

These curves are on the character skeleton, so whenever a Montage plays at runtime, it animates the character and camera simultaneously.

How curves are created[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/camera#how-curves-are-created)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You can find camera animations in each weapon’s **Camera** folder. These sequences aren’t used directly; they are processed by the extraction tool, which transfers camera curves to the character animation:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F1Aw4pkTl9tjQWS2zu9E9%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9a56e260-3781-456b-a66d-d33a3b5b730f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8ed65f7148878d24c7e2ad7280475dc3&sv=3)

Camera Motion Extractor.

**Tip**: Use this modifier to re‑extract or adjust the intensity of camera animations.First Person Camera

Actor Component applies camera animations by sampling curve values at runtime:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F6Ia3XS28Z0m7A9o1mVE8%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7023f02a-a238-479e-a4fe-f50bf24f392f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d449a736b8a3533bff3cd199a7f50130&sv=3)

Camera animation.

**Tip**: This component also plays camera recoil shakes.

# GASP Integration | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration.md)
.

Character meshes[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration#character-meshes)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**Note:** Compatible with GASP **5.4**, **5.5**, **5.6**. Add the latest Tactical Shooter Pack to your GASP project before starting.

First, we need to create a base for our integration. Duplicate the **BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter** (**KINEMATION/TacticalShooterPack/Blueprints**):

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FEab8rXK8OhDXL54KTvxw%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc714e553-ca9d-4ad0-9451-db77fc2c0d1b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0e99b49b5f7a136e1b23f3be5b337a23&sv=3)

New blueprint.

This will be our main player blueprint. Open it up, go to **Class Settings** and inherit it from the **CBP\_SandboxCharacter**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fej9njSE1kHGzr7E1h8P0%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De93a2089-5cdd-4711-aefa-e4163b91969d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4237acec219da1296342de30ace7502f&sv=3)

Change the parent class.

Next, select the "Mesh" and set its animation blueprint to **ABP\_SandboxCharacter** and skeletal mesh to **SKM\_UEFN\_Mannequin**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fr5EppNs1jsKwyXPw80ZB%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8cc3265c-c994-488a-884e-6fde84cce794&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=56ffc218f101dcd9b972872ca8d95ed2&sv=3)

Default mesh component.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Ft4VuVb99dW9W1m3AmYly%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da22c0580-cf73-4af6-a016-995cef7a38e2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fe5e72d876eec4104759871d370f4889&sv=3)

Properties for motion matching.

**Note**: The "Mesh" skeletal component will generate a motion-matching pose (full-body movement), which will be blended with our tactical animations.

Set **Visible** to **false** and **Visibility Based Anim Tick Option** to **Always Tick Pose and Refresh Bones**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FjImH4DBQ2lHEdM36zZjX%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D914992f5-ab39-4a50-8145-768d880d3470&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8c80721183c4d4e67d9a8f4aad90e8f0&sv=3)

Keep this mesh hidden.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FesRENIZS3aztVB81IvId%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df55a4e1e-ce74-4ae6-8e9b-8ed2a12a69f8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f9ca25ec9dfb912ab4856a4452171777&sv=3)

Always update animation, even if mesh is hidden.

Now we need to add a skeletal mesh for our character model. Let's call it "Player" and parent the **Camera** component to it:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FbBpzSzRqqTtOjcD2HePE%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D42bd1616-2dd1-4655-88fc-0b536f85af9d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8be6bae00053789c7f91dd1ba5c5ef9c&sv=3)

Camera is a first-person camera component.

In the **Camera** settings, set its parent socket to `FPCamera` and reset its transform:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FvE3UPE8L9WPGqnQgojDe%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0cb2cb09-9da0-491a-84b6-19f09d06ae2d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=178b8eb27d4b3a06efc2535e813baaa8&sv=3)

Camera settings.

Find the `GetPlayerMesh` interface function and return the new "Player" skeletal mesh component:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F1onRx0FGiMkoUHv3eSMN%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4e428402-9353-4ff7-895e-23a79806e706&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f85ade8e7861720d148ba3716da62074&sv=3)

Open up this function.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F2qz9YPmFycEyCkAJUJFQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7255936d-547c-4139-91ef-4ed99bd4ec76&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c56f617572bf55a66835c873a38705e7&sv=3)

Return the "Player", not the default "Mesh".

**Tip**: This interface function is used by weapons and animation blueprints to play animations, so make sure to provide the correct reference here.

Cameras[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration#cameras)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The second step is to properly set up cameras in our player blueprint.

If you use GASP 5.5 or 5.6, make sure to use this code snippet to disable other cameras:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FlFofXOOMNCO0iv9iWz7G%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc9920bd1-afc0-4b7f-ba9e-c57788705def&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ce24693ea051d33188ea1d61a13272e7&sv=3)

Place this in BeginPlay.

The **Camera** attached to **Player** is the main first‑person camera. Deactivate all other cameras that may be active at game start.

If you are on 5.4, then simply set all other cameras' `Auto Activate` property to **false**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FpCG7SW1wG7PudK3U0ktz%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6c4bb833-fd5e-47cb-8eae-f1e8d53490ca&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=57069a7645006c3903a4ed5d7a69c163&sv=3)

Disable other cameras.

Inputs[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration#inputs)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The next step is to properly update GASP inputs at runtime. Open the `Update Player Movement` function (it's called on **Tick**):

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FWUbmsp1zl6dpydDaVtsk%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deaa8b0c9-df35-4253-a837-d72aa411eb0e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e229d844773e3d031a185d6f0d221a97&sv=3)

Update movement function.

Find some place at the end of the function, and set the value of the Gait property based on Movement State:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FQQYXOyetTFwkXY9xvGo3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8bc23a45-8d27-450e-a1df-aeb3b365fae7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d6cfa393b5a7419103de5914332c3f3e&sv=3)

Updating current Gait.

**Note**: `Gait` is from **GASP**, and `Movement State` is a **Tactical Shooter Pack** property.

Then, we need to update `bWantsToSprint`, `bWantsToWalk`, `bWantsToAim` and `bWantsToStrafe` properties.

**Note:** The setup differs between **5.4** and **5.5/5.6**; refer to the images below.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FMiwEvhh4XXunpS1eDcBy%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D379e354d-2951-4c22-8306-5f930851212d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b39f5d17fe213b18101b805f22a8678a&sv=3)

5.4 version also requires the Update Rotation function to be called.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FXNJcaEAl7u7QZVg4QQkn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D28828ddc-609a-40e9-a97a-5ea6db1d67d1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fbf38b3dd8db49085758218ab3d6883d&sv=3)

5.4/5.6 version.

Animation blueprint[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration#animation-blueprint)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Now we need to create an animation blueprint (ABP) that will combine motion-matching pose and animations from the **Tactical Shooter Pack**.

Create a new Animation Blueprint using the **UE5 Manny** skeleton from the pack:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FbYPfPtihznedbGRK4oq1%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1cc08a3-afc6-429b-aa66-1dc940df0aa5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e7e1b312dfc6e1d56c08c34e89fe4e91&sv=3)

Creating a new animation blueprint.

Go to the Event Graph and use this snippet for initialization:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FOhBRSilQwqqLwJg9wAnq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0f78eac5-1836-4339-8731-b5672ecdf1b2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4483b7c30383558fa3388483d68bb565&sv=3)

Simple initialization logic.

**Tip**_:_ Obtain a reference to your player Blueprint here.

In the **Anim Graph**, add a **Retarget Pose From Mesh** node. Set the `Retarget From` to **Custom Skeletal Mesh Component**, and `IK Retargeter Asset` to **RTG\_UEFN\_to\_UE5\_Mannequin**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fiu4clvunWjSQStc0XinS%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D82b29459-c14a-483d-b208-5fb41277dbc7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ed5c23c65f2b671e134ddbab8e96e734&sv=3)

Retargeting settings.

After that, bind the `Source Mesh Component` using the "Mesh" property from the player blueprint.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FgC9FZjqvIC0XdFwiNf8Q%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8b3ca6fe-4583-4a1d-b0a7-68e7a2ed7c7d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d6dfe6e2f3ae0e7b8ddbc24032e6b270&sv=3)

Bind the "Mesh" here.

Next, add the ABP\_TacticalShooter\_UE5 linked anim instance and blend it with the retargeted pose:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FECVoSVrc5T1ewF6R9fi7%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D55a4796e-00cc-4db8-8ed5-52a001718858&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c789fc16f03b0348ce087c3447a60df3&sv=3)

Simple blending setup.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FxGGlTX4W3LB6MbTvsJZR%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D145b5ed9-3543-48f6-9a4f-aa46b1c81caa&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1021ba0a342656ecc3b9af4443632b12&sv=3)

Override the upper body.

**Tip**: Feed the retargeted pose into **Base Pose**, and Tactical Shooter animations into **Blend Pose 0**.

Parkour adjustments[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration#parkour-adjustments)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To properly blend parkour animations, we need to adjust our layering. First, create 2 cached poses:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FhJUGVmudxeK418M7f4AK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D64e7ba04-0121-4c16-a462-4150d088f6bf&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=80ff680f7cb413dd7573b78f4992e60a&sv=3)

Cached poses.

**UpperBody** carries Tactical Shooter animations; **MotionMatchingPose** carries full‑body motion.

Next, add a new **Layered Blend Per Bone** node:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FXoWy5AJlXPxhVBVF77Do%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7119e17a-40b7-42f4-88ee-a5452b306c0f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5c482ebfd265abdd5f236a317819e930&sv=3)

This pose will be used when climbing.

Add a **Blend by Bool** node and plug the layered result into **True:**

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F7JBnucvb75WwLF9IMxRc%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcc34a872-f503-40a9-908c-77b55897b519&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c622e679ffae568a50c8931c3ae5d917&sv=3)

Use the "Upper Body" as a "False Pose".

This will blend between parkour and normal poses using the `DoingTraversalAction` boolean from our character blueprint.

Finally, blend the result with the "MotionMatchingPose":

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FhRVsxJoyE20zW1PzFijH%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df494d262-1cb6-4834-b144-19f02947fe96&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=98063b7e9398e3895618d669ea85ebb2&sv=3)

Final blending step.

# Player | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player.md)
.

Overview[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player#overview)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter** is a game‑ready player Blueprint responsible for managing weapons, inputs, and actions:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FIXA7np59yB2VCQlyoaXK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dae4e0207-2e6f-471a-8892-db337f861ba1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=85847b5a57c68b4735c2b3520dfa3578&sv=3)

Tactical Shooter Character.

[🔷First Person Camera](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/first-person-camera)
[🔷Tactical Shooter Animation](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/tactical-shooter-animation)
[🔷IK Motion Player](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/ik-motion-player)

Character Settings[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player#character-settings)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**TacticalCharacterSettings** is a Data Asset that tells the player Blueprint which meshes, Animation Blueprints, and weapons to use:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F2j5lvCP1eVWXFD785SBG%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3a3e8bf0-c899-4ecb-9a3f-e807491f25ae&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3b58183593c7dc0e30d85492448a7afd&sv=3)

Character Settings.

The pack supports switching between UE4 and UE5 meshes at runtime, so you can specify different skeletons and Animation Blueprints. By default, the UE5 mesh is used.

The **Weapons** list contains weapon Blueprint classes used by the character.

BPI Tactical Shooter Character[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player#bpi-tactical-shooter-character)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This Blueprint Interface connects the player with animation systems and third‑party systems:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FdGxpWuJtRLCKxDmjhkPn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8024b36b-81b3-49c4-b8c6-576ab837b916&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5464ed1a689dbc2f7d272806237b5262&sv=3)

Interface functions.

*   **Get Player Mesh:** Returns the player's **Skeletal Mesh Component**.
    
*   **Get Primary Weapon:** Returns the currently equipped weapon. When quick‑drawing a **sidearm**, it returns the pistol.
    
*   **Get Main Weapon:** Returns the main weapon.
    
*   **Get Tactical Shooter Component:** Returns the `AC_TacticalShooterAnimation` component.
    

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FnBlm7SPaxCztlxMC4XFc%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Debe94dc9-d721-448b-be70-082333613a60&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=87895b3f1ecec321af8f22f18508870d&sv=3)

Returns the player mesh here.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FkLxdhhit3Kv6k6b0Q2TQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D19c00b97-4dc5-4ece-a618-843c869f1332&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ac368cdd86485d3e9e3aefe278388a31&sv=3)

Returns the currently used weapon.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fw4wCxdqSwqNQNfG53qbu%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc8823f93-4f9e-4b6b-b319-2dc02973b88b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7d748874f9a453a0060bd7859696f096&sv=3)

Returns the current weapon.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FoAeXoRj1WTYdLppiHsjY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1a5828c4-00d7-484f-9bf8-93faf95e2780&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8b7cc9c361647c3370be08c7a75009f3&sv=3)

Returns a reference to the animation component.

Input Actions[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player#input-actions)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

All Input Actions are implemented in this Blueprint. Here is the list of actions used in the pack:

*   **Weapon-specific**
    
    *   Reload
        
    *   Mag Check
        
    *   Inspect
        
    *   Fire
        
    
*   **General**
    
    *   Aiming
        
    *   Change Weapon
        
    *   Change Fire Mode
        
    *   Look
        
    *   Move
        
    *   Sprint
        
    *   Free Look
        
    *   Pistol Quick Draw
        
    *   Lean

# Procedural Recoil | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil.md)
.

Recoil Animation[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#recoil-animation)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This Actor Component generates smooth weapon‑firing animation at runtime:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F2cJKtq71awQ6dVWVy2IK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D91402215-4c60-42d9-b510-973e6d49cb58&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d35508a5bc93e575c1dcdc8e80605a39&sv=3)

Recoil Animation component.

It is typically added to the character Blueprint, but you can also add it to the weapon. Call its methods as described below.

### Init[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#init)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FBqFBP1WfEQA6KxY8tWOI%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D90919f99-c495-4389-b0a3-c5652296f078&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=71da8759cdea6994c4455f0df84a89ba&sv=3)

Init method.

Use this function to update active recoil settings. It requires a **Recoil Data Asset** and **Fire Rate** (rounds per minute).

### Play/Stop[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#play-stop)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FnfIETHvkFkA3EDxgpCBJ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dad47378a-b358-4802-9b12-2c9312669246&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=76be76e03909b2717000db76dc3e11e8&sv=3)

Play, Stop functions.

Call **Play** whenever a shot is fired, and **Stop** when the fire input is released.

### Set Aiming[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#set-aiming)

Use this method to update the current aiming state:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FzZdyRQBjENywlGllIcX0%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D172b83bf-25c1-4730-bc0f-172880c7a9ea&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c78163a69f60975d4b4dd8f09ffd2da&sv=3)

Set Aiming.

### Set Fire Mode[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#set-fire-mode)

Use this method to update the current fire mode:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Flni1JsKFbdxUiEB7tYkE%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D42383d96-6e53-47f3-97cd-c6365f3e948b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0adcf55c8218671ab5dd89b7fb9ded9d&sv=3)

Set Fire Mode.

Three fire modes are supported:

*   Semi
    
*   Burst
    
*   Auto
    

**Note:** For Recoil Animation, there is no technical difference between **Burst** and **Auto**.

Recoil Animation Data[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#recoil-animation-data)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This Data Asset contains recoil properties, from simple inputs to complex layers such as **Sway** and **Pushback**.

### Input values[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#input-values)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fz9Jr2W2fnQDlckv9K82G%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D95992f06-7169-4a89-8288-d96e22dbffac&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6fe9127e9f49fee78b302f1daca16cfd&sv=3)

Input Hip values.

**Hip** and **Aim** properties define recoil ranges. For **Yaw** and **Roll**, a `FVector4` avoids values too close to zero. Final value is computed as:

Pseudocode

Copy

    Yaw = Random(Random(YawHip.X, YawHip.Y), Random(YawHip.Z, YawHip.W));

### Controller Recoil[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#controller-recoil)

Adds pitch and yaw to the controller rotation:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F7DBKImowRQX00JwwQ5ya%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D92bc5d80-b7b6-4553-bf74-6ec7735b5ba4&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=081ce57f48f9b34fcc906d5a84c8ef0c&sv=3)

Controller Recoil

*   **Horizontal Recoil Step**: Value range for the yaw input.
    
*   **Vertical Recoil Step**: Value range for the pitch input.
    
*   **Interp Speed**: Recoil smoothing speed.
    
*   **Damping**: How fast recoil should recover.
    
*   **Compensation**: How much player input is used for compensation. **1** uses all input; **0** uses none (the weapon settles after firing).
    

### Recoil Noise[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#recoil-noise)

Random **Y-Z** (right/up) movement that adds translation noise when firing:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FaKu45EsBf7G1xVBd2Gig%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Daebffd1f-dae7-49a3-b332-dedd0ba9fb20&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a944a813a8837172a75e8138feae6940&sv=3)

Noise layer.

*   **Target**: Left and Up target values - these will be added every shot.
    
*   **Interp Speed**: Smoothing speed.
    
*   **Damping**: How fast noise should go to zero.
    
*   **Aim Scalar**: Multiplier applied when aiming.
    

### Pushback[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#pushback)

This layer is applied on the second shot of a burst and auto sequence:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Ff00MF32swAmS089tIg5d%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D41ef793f-b28d-4bc9-8aa5-51b8311b94ad&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0162aa8c0427144b4fe94c78f2807269&sv=3)

**Pushback** layer.

*   **Amount**: How much backward translation to apply.
    
*   **Damping**: How fast the value should go to zero.
    
*   **Interp Speed**: General smoothing speed.
    

### Progress[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#progress)

Accumulates **pitch** and **upward translation** with every shot:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F0E2pSxP8HPTdvcwPBIsY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db9065848-253d-4f78-b0c1-a3cbbc8849f4&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a175822415012b437d823788317b979c&sv=3)

Progress layer.

*   **Pitch Interp Speed**: Smoothing pitch speed.
    
*   **Pitch Damping**: How fast pitch should go to zero.
    
*   **Pitch Amount**: How much pitch to add with each shot.
    
*   **Up Interp Speed**: Smoothing up speed.
    
*   **Up Damping**: How fast the up translation should go to zero.
    
*   **Up Amount**: How much up translation to add with each shot.
    
*   **Ads Scale**: Multiplier applied when aiming.
    

### Sway[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#sway)

**Recoil Sway** simulates arm offset when firing:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FxV7iltxEmw3UoQV7JWcY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddb7602d3-f68c-42b9-9952-f06c04fc150d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4c0519732a257d8e52cc626b809fce1b&sv=3)

Sway properties.

*   **Pitch Sway**: A value from this range will be added to the pitch sway.
    
*   **Yaw Sway**: A value from this range will be added to the yaw sway.
    
*   **Roll Sway Multiplier**: Roll will be computed by multiplying the pitch sway with this value.
    
*   **Damping**: How fast the sway should go to zero.
    
*   **Interp Speed**: Sway smoothing speed.
    
*   **Ads Scale**: Multiplier applied when aiming.
    
*   **Pivot Offset**: Virtual pivot to add translation. Recommended **X = 30-40** units so the pivot is near the character’s shoulder.
    

### General[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#general)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FidPhUgL1ZPFKOIN95haK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dbd4dc4a8-d1d2-4938-a721-c4a5b1fd9844&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b40a96726f301bf5d13d1034c2e0c2ff&sv=3)

General recoil properties.

**Tip**: Pivot Offsets apply recoil as if the weapon rotates around a point in world space. For example, with `(-30, 0, 0)`, recoil appears to pivot around the gun offset by that amount.

*   **Space Rotation**: Use only if animations have a different root orientation.
    
*   **Play rate**: Playback speed multiplier.
    

**Playback Offset** applies only to burst or auto fire modes. In these modes, recoil animation length equals the fire delay (`60 / FireRate`). **Playback Offset** adjusts that length to make the sequence longer or shorter.

### Curves[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil#curves)

Recoil animation is curve‑driven. The solver uses **Vector Curves** for single and auto/burst fire modes:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FEZoH388YNkxVJgnt5tVn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db38e978a-433e-46ec-8a96-1e3f35255d24&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=67862513e976981ba018d3a0e5312ebd&sv=3)

Recoil curves.

**Tip**: For single‑fire‑only weapons, set **AutoRot** and **AutoLoc** to **None**.How to get recoil values

To get recoil values, access the `FTransform RecoilAnimation` property:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FnGRQkUAqDbmPZ0Jr6iEC%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D10c4d35a-7599-4ddf-94cf-8db0220f08ca&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ebc9882283cf7b66be5b336d24992c84&sv=3)

Recoil value.

**Tip**: For example, plug this value into a **Transform (Modify) Bone** node in the Animation Blueprint.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FI9lRznqkFlgBePMa7Gp7%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8f0dedc3-b042-43f1-91a2-83e37e2b2d2f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=23cd9362a084c0e6be4fd276df85cd25&sv=3)

How recoil is applied in the Tactial Shooter Pack.

# How to change character model | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/how-to-change-character-model.md)
.

1

### Assign the skeleton[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/how-to-change-character-model#assign-the-skeleton)

Right-click on your character mesh and click "Assign Skeleton". Next, pick the **Manny** skeleton from the Tactical Shooter Pack.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fq5LoXo0HpY9BBvovTbba%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De47b6188-687f-4127-9d34-9f6dbf734195&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e31143eabb527693811d64dcba56d83d&sv=3)

**Note**: This will only work with models rigged to the UE5 Mannequin.

2

### Create a new Animation Blueprint[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/how-to-change-character-model#create-a-new-animation-blueprint)

Pick the **Manny** skeleton from the Tactical Shooter Pack.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fn56plOo0FR5il78xBDmA%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db03f9b90-5ea8-4138-8ae1-273d82ee5db9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=86bff17addf9ba7c0d5d7667eeacaaa5&sv=3)

Open the blueprint, go to "Class Defaults" and set "Parent Class" to "ABP\_CopyPose".

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FOYfZDIKwyob0qOdyl2fq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc312e22d-9b00-4b3c-a3dd-f2ea32166655&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e2a0d473188d591b29b0cb8796e1d9b0&sv=3)

3

### Add a new mesh component[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/how-to-change-character-model#add-a-new-mesh-component)

Go to the `BP_TacticalShooterCharacter` blueprint, add a new Skeletal Mesh Component and parent it to "Mesh".

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FvcHq5M6rfgMSirlF766z%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd8b9d167-d2a3-46e7-88b4-e3479d3ceb4b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9f581744a2bdd35ed90ab008b34a7af6&sv=3)

4

### Assign the mesh and blueprint[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/how-to-change-character-model#assign-the-mesh-and-blueprint)

Select the new mesh component and assign your custom character mesh and the animation blueprint.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FGO4WwERXwiLg96yVlGO1%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D38d95380-5838-4cce-a406-4acf9b3d6d40&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3139d4ba0e05db388454cdb9d0e1140f&sv=3)

5

### Adjust the main mesh[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/how-to-change-character-model#adjust-the-main-mesh)

Finally, select the "Mesh" component and set its visibility flag to false.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FDJGj6usUJSNKDB7K1cm1%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D24e988f4-d775-4580-90fe-35a8bcc30d3f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=981bdc95e11cb87fe6b11c5089f84ff8&sv=3)

Search for "Anim Tick" and set it to "Always Tick Pose and Refresh Bones".

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FniXHEEVYEGt7Lt8uFOPk%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddd9851e9-c68f-4215-89b4-ea73d81ecd16&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d1157305e3239987789f30dd0ee8aea9&sv=3)
