# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/overview.md).

# Overview

## Content

All content - animations, weapons, sounds, and Blueprints - is located in \*\*KINEMATION/TacticalShooterPack\*\*.:

<figure><img src="/files/kdFskrhBRXRU0UJv4hza" alt="" width="483"><figcaption><p>Tactical Shooter Pack content.</p></figcaption></figure>

The \*\*Common\*\* directory contains general Blueprints used across KINEMATION assets. Its contents are covered later in this section.

The Tactical Shooter Pack consists of \*\*11\*\* directories:

<figure><img src="/files/5F7yzoDxnpv4wOuGnQ7q" alt=""><figcaption><p>Content folders.</p></figcaption></figure>

Let's break down each sub-folder to better explain what you can find there.

## Animations

This folder contains camera, character, and weapon animations. It also includes Animation Blueprints for <mark style="color:yellow;">UE4</mark> and <mark style="color:yellow;">UE5</mark> skeletons.

<figure><img src="/files/x8MYG0zXZIzI9bbB0P72" alt="" width="394"><figcaption><p>Animation Blueprints for UE5 Mannequin.</p></figcaption></figure>

The main Animation Blueprint is \*\*ABP\\\_TacticalShooter\\\_UE5\*\*, which relies on these modules:

\* \*\*ABP\\\_UE5\\\_Sway\*\*: Applies procedural aiming and movement sway.
\* \*\*ABP\\\_UE5\\\_IK\\\_Ads\*\*: Applies procedural aiming.
\* \*\*ABP\\\_UE5\\\_IK\\\_Main\*\*: Applies general procedural animation (spine rotation, leaning, recoil, etc.).
\* \*\*ABP\\\_UE5\\\_IK\\\_Pose\*\*: Positions the weapon and hands via IK.

All Animation Blueprints depend on \*\*AC\\\_TacticalShooterAnimation -\*\* the central component in this asset. It supplies ABPs with key data (e.g., IK settings and firing state).

## Blueprints

This folder contains all Blueprints used in the pack:

<figure><img src="/files/BzSG5pxS0KsBms2oiuGy" alt="" width="480"><figcaption><p>Blueprint content.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*AdditiveMotions\*\*: Procedural additive animations played when aiming or changing fire mode.
\* \*\*Attachments\*\*: Blueprint foundation for the attachment system \*(work in progress)\*.
\* \*\*Data\*\*: Settings and structs used in the pack (e.g., weapon animation settings).
\* \*\*Notifies\*\*: Custom Animation Notifies used in the pack.
\* \*\*Recoil\*\*: Recoil settings for each weapon.
\* \*\*Weapons\*\*: Game‑ready weapon Blueprints.

This folder also contains very important Blueprints, like:

<figure><img src="/files/k9jMaAjufB4z3wvSCiHw" alt="" width="473"><figcaption><p>Blueprint content.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*AC\\\_TacticalShooterAnimation:\*\* Actor Component that connects gameplay and Animation Blueprints; treat it as an interface between the player and the animation system.
\* \*\*BP\\\_TacticalShooterCharacter:\*\* Game‑ready player Blueprint.
\* \*\*BP\\\_TacticalShooterWeapon:\*\* Base class for all weapons.
\* \*\*BPI\\\_TacticalShooterCharacter:\*\* Interface used by weapons and Animation Blueprints.
\* \*\*BPI\\\_TacticalShooterWeapon:\*\* Interface that passes data to the animation system.
\* \*\*TacOperatorSettings\*\* is an asset that contains meshes as well as weapons to use.

## Camera, Casings, Character and Weapons

This section contains all skeletal and static meshes used in the package. The \*\*Weapons\*\* folder also includes attachments (e.g., magazines).

## Demo

This directory includes a simple demo scene and the UE5 Manny.

## Inputs

This folder contains all \*\*Input Actions\*\* and the \*\*Input Mapping Context\*\* used by the player Blueprint.

## SFX

This folder contains all sound effects and sound Blueprints. Weapon‑specific sounds are divided into two categories: \*\*(1)\*\* Actions (e.g., reloads, inspects, mag checks) and \*\*(2)\*\* Firing.

## UI

This folder contains a UMG widget used in the playable demo. It displays the control list and the currently equipped weapon.

## VFX

This folder contains Data Assets for the Niagara casing system. Each asset is set up for a specific casing (e.g., NATO 5.56, 9 mm).

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player.md).

# Player

## Overview

\*\*BP\\\_TacticalShooterCharacter\*\* is a game‑ready player Blueprint responsible for managing weapons, inputs, and actions:

<figure><img src="/files/K1LfoxAggojVBvhjn1ZC" alt="" width="389"><figcaption><p>Tactical Shooter Character.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% content-ref url="/pages/5quB0w7sPcANQ6yZowKV" %}
\[First Person Camera\](/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/first-person-camera.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

{% content-ref url="/pages/F7XaHTeRkZMkzwoseNWU" %}
\[Tactical Shooter Animation\](/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/tactical-shooter-animation.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

{% content-ref url="/pages/l4Zujom9GysG7rVYThgR" %}
\[IK Motion Player\](/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/ik-motion-player.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

## Character Settings

\*\*TacticalCharacterSettings\*\* is a Data Asset that tells the player Blueprint which meshes, Animation Blueprints, and weapons to use:

<figure><img src="/files/wQPbTsbNAbfYnGdLeAcO" alt="" width="422"><figcaption><p>Character Settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

The pack supports switching between UE4 and UE5 meshes at runtime, so you can specify different skeletons and Animation Blueprints. By default, the UE5 mesh is used.

The \*\*Weapons\*\* list contains weapon Blueprint classes used by the character.

## BPI Tactical Shooter Character

This Blueprint Interface connects the player with animation systems and third‑party systems:

<figure><img src="/files/eF1RqeIYuFdvlzKE2TG1" alt="" width="220"><figcaption><p>Interface functions.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Get Player Mesh:\*\* Returns the player's \*\*Skeletal Mesh Component\*\*.
\* \*\*Get Primary Weapon:\*\* Returns the currently equipped weapon. When quick‑drawing a \*\*sidearm\*\*, it returns the pistol.
\* \*\*Get Main Weapon:\*\* Returns the main weapon.
\* \*\*Get Tactical Shooter Component:\*\* Returns the \`AC\_TacticalShooterAnimation\` component.

<figure><img src="/files/AQDkaKQ0yBe7A4oxNnB0" alt="" width="410"><figcaption><p>Returns the player mesh here.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/Kq60d2NrkP3kyJBZIuIN" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Returns the currently used weapon.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/zcJlugv0F0MxV3Gf3Y8r" alt="" width="344"><figcaption><p>Returns the current weapon.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/ZpMd2UqcEUPjPdgHZjgt" alt="" width="520"><figcaption><p>Returns a reference to the animation component.</p></figcaption></figure>

## Input Actions

All Input Actions are implemented in this Blueprint. Here is the list of actions used in the pack:

\* \*\*Weapon-specific\*\*
  \* Reload
  \* Mag Check
  \* Inspect
  \* Fire
\* \*\*General\*\*
  \* Aiming
  \* Change Weapon
  \* Change Fire Mode
  \* Look
  \* Move
  \* Sprint
  \* Free Look
  \* Pistol Quick Draw
  \* Lean

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration.md).

# GASP Integration

## Character meshes

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Note:\*\* Compatible with GASP \*\*5.4\*\*, \*\*5.5\*\*, \*\*5.6\*\*. Add the latest Tactical Shooter Pack to your GASP project before starting.
{% endhint %}

First, we need to create a base for our integration. Duplicate the \*\*BP\\\_TacticalShooterCharacter\*\* (<mark style="background-color:purple;">\*\*KINEMATION/TacticalShooterPack/Blueprints\*\*</mark>):

<figure><img src="/files/11pgqsetFPfBryn1jazM" alt="" width="102"><figcaption><p>New blueprint.</p></figcaption></figure>

This will be our main player blueprint. Open it up, go to \*\*Class Settings\*\* and inherit it from the \*\*CBP\\\_SandboxCharacter\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/bN5CMmO44j2CPpjHb5ni" alt=""><figcaption><p>Change the parent class.</p></figcaption></figure>

Next, select the "Mesh" and set its animation blueprint to \*\*ABP\\\_SandboxCharacter\*\* and skeletal mesh to \*\*SKM\\\_UEFN\\\_Mannequin\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/HLg6aOArNXHGfGNXOxTK" alt="" width="244"><figcaption><p>Default mesh component.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/awsAJOsUuKAFFgfFB2Uk" alt="" width="434"><figcaption><p>Properties for motion matching.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Note\*\*: The "Mesh" skeletal component will generate a motion-matching pose (full-body movement), which will be blended with our tactical animations.
{% endhint %}

Set \*\*Visible\*\* to \*\*false\*\* and \*\*Visibility Based Anim Tick Option\*\* to \*\*Always Tick Pose and Refresh Bones\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/0tYvMH9SSBj4tqN0UfTH" alt="" width="238"><figcaption><p>Keep this mesh hidden.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/BkCmJWX77ElMUlZaWBfV" alt="" width="434"><figcaption><p>Always update animation, even if mesh is hidden.</p></figcaption></figure>

Now we need to add a skeletal mesh for our character model. Let's call it "Player" and parent the \*\*Camera\*\* component to it:

<figure><img src="/files/5fixAFGjRAvXTEri3C6D" alt="" width="415"><figcaption><p>Camera is a first-person camera component.</p></figcaption></figure>

In the \*\*Camera\*\* settings, set its parent socket to \`FPCamera\` and reset its transform:

<figure><img src="/files/G5Qr3wmd2IykH85fnO8l" alt="" width="434"><figcaption><p>Camera settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

Find the \`GetPlayerMesh\` interface function and return the new "Player" skeletal mesh component:

<figure><img src="/files/BxbVl9xYzt9xJKbhqOgP" alt="" width="277"><figcaption><p>Open up this function.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/k9dVqdSHlLRJ7HmVoh2T" alt="" width="392"><figcaption><p>Return the "Player", not the default "Mesh".</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: This interface function is used by weapons and animation blueprints to play animations, so make sure to provide the correct reference here.
{% endhint %}

## Cameras

The second step is to properly set up cameras in our player blueprint.

If you use GASP 5.5 or 5.6, make sure to use this code snippet to disable other cameras:

<figure><img src="/files/maQY3hQk4zFMUnKhvbwS" alt=""><figcaption><p>Place this in BeginPlay.</p></figcaption></figure>

The \*\*Camera\*\* attached to \*\*Player\*\* is the main first‑person camera. Deactivate all other cameras that may be active at game start.

If you are on 5.4, then simply set all other cameras' \`Auto Activate\` property to \*\*false\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/LhGkHOMtQKsB8dmKBBtB" alt="" width="205"><figcaption><p>Disable other cameras.</p></figcaption></figure>

## Inputs

The next step is to properly update GASP inputs at runtime. Open the \`Update Player Movement\` function (it's called on \*\*Tick\*\*):

<figure><img src="/files/1ddSCH7gnVcBfIW5kOBO" alt="" width="483"><figcaption><p>Update movement function.</p></figcaption></figure>

Find some place at the end of the function, and set the value of the Gait property based on Movement State:

<figure><img src="/files/53QoiphVkf5wvW2w7Y4D" alt="" width="377"><figcaption><p>Updating current Gait.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Note\*\*: \`Gait\` is from \*\*GASP\*\*, and \`Movement State\` is a \*\*Tactical Shooter Pack\*\* property.
{% endhint %}

Then, we need to update \`bWantsToSprint\`, \`bWantsToWalk\`, \`bWantsToAim\` and \`bWantsToStrafe\` properties.

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Note:\*\* The setup differs between \*\*5.4\*\* and \*\*5.5/5.6\*\*; refer to the images below.
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/y4XRAirYu8xYXnNj01Tp" alt=""><figcaption><p>5.4 version also requires the Update Rotation function to be called.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/a8q5vmu2KGNyoRO55qvu" alt=""><figcaption><p>5.4/5.6 version.</p></figcaption></figure>

## Animation blueprint

Now we need to create an animation blueprint (ABP) that will combine motion-matching pose and animations from the \*\*Tactical Shooter Pack\*\*.

Create a new Animation Blueprint using the \*\*UE5 Manny\*\* skeleton from the pack:

<figure><img src="/files/kaEZTKbKXlE1uTj3ttdU" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Creating a new animation blueprint.</p></figcaption></figure>

Go to the Event Graph and use this snippet for initialization:

<figure><img src="/files/4rP0UelaBgKZdoibuDe8" alt=""><figcaption><p>Simple initialization logic.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*\*:\* Obtain a reference to your player Blueprint here.
{% endhint %}

In the \*\*Anim Graph\*\*, add a \*\*Retarget Pose From Mesh\*\* node. Set the \`Retarget From\` to \*\*Custom Skeletal Mesh Component\*\*, and \`IK Retargeter Asset\` to \*\*RTG\\\_UEFN\\\_to\\\_UE5\\\_Mannequin\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/EMcoXAu4jyNkWe4BCH7Q" alt="" width="556"><figcaption><p>Retargeting settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

After that, bind the \`Source Mesh Component\` using the "Mesh" property from the player blueprint.

<figure><img src="/files/VBbzJ4h8HvARthEdFQ2i" alt="" width="265"><figcaption><p>Bind the "Mesh" here.</p></figcaption></figure>

Next, add the ABP\\\_TacticalShooter\\\_UE5 linked anim instance and blend it with the retargeted pose:

<figure><img src="/files/WmmOtJn7k1i1uQH6xdhH" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Simple blending setup.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/xg9W9vbZW1tTJZSYgLv3" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Override the upper body.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: Feed the retargeted pose into \*\*Base Pose\*\*, and Tactical Shooter animations into \*\*Blend Pose 0\*\*.
{% endhint %}

## Parkour adjustments

To properly blend parkour animations, we need to adjust our layering. First, create 2 cached poses:

<figure><img src="/files/H7QoC3bNEHFk0uGlYZp6" alt="" width="449"><figcaption><p>Cached poses.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*UpperBody\*\* carries Tactical Shooter animations; \*\*MotionMatchingPose\*\* carries full‑body motion.

Next, add a new \*\*Layered Blend Per Bone\*\* node:

<figure><img src="/files/vIofnoH1qhZa3gdpQRUk" alt=""><figcaption><p>This pose will be used when climbing.</p></figcaption></figure>

Add a \*\*Blend by Bool\*\* node and plug the layered result into \*\*True:\*\*

<figure><img src="/files/myKsp3uVzPTdmLOa1YsH" alt="" width="524"><figcaption><p>Use the "Upper Body" as a "False Pose".</p></figcaption></figure>

This will blend between parkour and normal poses using the \`DoingTraversalAction\` boolean from our character blueprint.

Finally, blend the result with the "MotionMatchingPose":

<figure><img src="/files/YYNN3IudvpYPyBVRq81q" alt=""><figcaption><p>Final blending step.</p></figcaption></figure>

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/character.md).

# Character

## How it's animated

All animations target the \*\*UE5 Mannequin\*\* skeleton and rely on Epic’s IK bone set:

<figure><img src="/files/N6meWTdKoNlTFVDDeH29" alt="" width="233"><figcaption><p>IK Bones</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\*\* drives weapon movement..
\* \*\*ik\\\_hand\\\_r\*\* contains right‑hand animation data.
\* \*\*ik\\\_hand\\\_l\*\* contains left‑hand animation data.

The setup also uses \*\*Virtual Bones (VBs)\*\* for procedural animation:

<figure><img src="/files/tjsjr7dzYaEoxXN1fGKt" alt="" width="202"><figcaption><p>Virtual Bone setup.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\*\* (targets \*\*ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\*\*): Used to position the pistol during quick draw so the \*\*sidearm\*\* is correctly placed relative to the \*\*right hand\*\*.
\* \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\\\_pivot\*\* (targets \*\*ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\*\*): All weapons are attached to this bone.
\* \*\*VB hand\\\_r/l\*\* (targets \*\*hand\\\_r/l\*\*): IK targets for the right/left hand.
\* \*\*VB lowerarm\\\_r/l\*\* (targets \*\*lowerarm\\\_r/l\*\*): Hint/pole targets for the right/left arm.
\* \*\*VB recoil\\\_hand\\\_r\*\* (targets \*\*root\*\*): Preserves right‑hand recoil when holding a pistol one‑handed.
\* \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\\\_pose\*\* (targets \*\*root\*\*): Used to compute the aiming pose.

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: Weapons are not parented to the right hand; they attach to \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\\\_pivot\*\*. This bone is blended in the Animation Blueprints so the final pose looks correct.
{% endhint %}

## Animation Blueprints

The pack uses modular \*\*Animation Blueprints\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/x8MYG0zXZIzI9bbB0P72" alt="" width="394"><figcaption><p>Animation Blueprints used in the pack.</p></figcaption></figure>

### ABP\\\_TacticalShooter\\\_UE5

This is the main Animation Blueprint; it incorporates the other animation assets. Here’s how it works:

#### Base character pose

<figure><img src="/files/hg4CkcgNzqGw9GXBilVE" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Base pose.</p></figcaption></figure>

The base combines a static idle \*\*Anim Sequence\*\* with the main \*\*Anim Montage\*\* (\*\*DefaultSlot\*\*).

#### Curve-based animations

<figure><img src="/files/oRt38uPqdRnyU5A7CnvS" alt=""><figcaption><p>Procedural movement graph.</p></figcaption></figure>

General movement (idle, walk, sprint, jump) is curve‑driven and procedural, applied primarily via \*\*Apply Additive\*\* nodes.

The \*\*Grounded\*\* and \*\*InAir\*\* states provide animation curves such as:

\* \*\*IK\\\_X\*\*/\*\*Y\*\*/\*\*Z\*\*: position.
\* \*\*IK\\\_Pitch\*\*/\*\*Yaw\*\*/\*\*Roll\*\*: rotation.
\* \*\*Camera Pitch\*\*/\*\*Yaw\*\*/\*\*Roll\*\*: camera animation.

These curves are layered on top of the base pose. Finally, we blend \*\*curve values\*\* (not poses) using a helper bone:

<figure><img src="/files/ZqYGnwGRXpjJYVEg8ADq" alt=""><figcaption><p>Procedural movement blending.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*VB Curves\*\* is a dummy bone used solely to blend curve values - not to animate a pose.

#### Pistol quick draw

This layer applies quick‑draw and holster animations additively on top of the character pose:

<figure><img src="/files/lDlZfCINY5J8qxIsbOGV" alt=""><figcaption><p>Pistol Quick Draw layer.</p></figcaption></figure>

Computes an additive pose per weapon, letting a single animation work across different poses.

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: Originally authored for \*\*TR15\*\*, but reused across weapons via this layer.Left hand actions
{% endhint %}

Apply left‑hand actions (e.g., take an item, push a door):

<figure><img src="/files/3XH0BmOlzePjrTlevNiB" alt="" width="160"><figcaption><p>Left Hand Actions layer.</p></figcaption></figure>

#### Finger firing motion

Animate the right index finger while firing:

<figure><img src="/files/S4EnLJcMYb6lPRAmQPby" alt="" width="467"><figcaption><p>Finger firing motion.</p></figcaption></figure>

A float smoothly interpolates to \*\*1\*\* while firing to rotate the finger, then returns to \*\*0\*\* afterward.

### ABP\\\_IK\\\_Pose

Positions the weapon and hands before the main procedural pass. It works in stages:

#### VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\\\_r

First, override \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\\\_r\*\* with the base idle pose when quick‑draw is enabled:

<figure><img src="/files/xKJ2MSzkW1UhysE5S6NY" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>VB ik\_hand\_gun\_r override.</p></figcaption></figure>

This is important for positioning the pistol properly.

#### Copying the animation data

Copy the animation data from real bones to VBs. This lets us apply IK additively instead of \*\*overriding\*\* and breaking the pose:

<figure><img src="/files/85DXF51hxJakbvIwwzrq" alt="" width="482"><figcaption><p>Copying the animation data.</p></figcaption></figure>

Then override \*\*VB hand\\\_r\*\* and \*\*VB hand\\\_l\*\* with the current grip pose:

<figure><img src="/files/TFFPXu2d0QnTqK04z7AM" alt=""><figcaption><p>Left hand pose override.</p></figcaption></figure>

By default, all weapons attach the left hand to the gun. You can smoothly disable this via a curve - for example, many animations \*\*do this\*\* to let the left hand move freely:

<figure><img src="/files/mf72ntL3RrUgF0jlMfQe" alt="" width="337"><figcaption><p><strong>Mask\_Grip</strong> can disable the left‑hand attachment.</p></figcaption></figure>

#### Offsets

Finally, apply \*\*FTransform\*\* offsets to all IK bones.

### ABP\\\_IK\\\_Ads

Applies procedural aiming in two steps:

1. Snap the weapon to the camera socket.
2. Apply the \*\*Aim Point\*\* local transform.

Note: The \*\*FPCamera\*\* socket is attached to \*\*neck\\\_02\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/xaeXsUdnR3lTZESIkjmv" alt=""><figcaption><p>Camera socket.</p></figcaption></figure>

### ABP\\\_IK\\\_Sway

Applies aiming and movement sway to \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\*\*. Sway is computed in \*\*AC\\\_TacticalShooterAnimation\*\*; this layer simply \*\*retrieves\*\* the \*\*FTransform\*\* and applies it to the bone:

<figure><img src="/files/GqjqygstVfRtFJoL3iIx" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Sway layer.</p></figcaption></figure>

### ABP\\\_IK\\\_Main

The final Animation Blueprint, responsible for:

1. Recoil
2. IK Motions
3. Leaning and Aim Offset
4. IK
5. Hiding the head bone

#### Recoil

<figure><img src="/files/yVUGTRurdzvKpEO628Sj" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>How recoil is applied.</p></figcaption></figure>

Recoil animation values are \*\*retrieved\*\* from the \*\*Recoil Animation\*\* component and applied via \*\*Transform (Modify) Bone\*\*. We preserve the right‑hand pose before applying recoil (needed for pistol quick draw).

#### IK Motions

<figure><img src="/files/Pa0yipk0H6QgrKnrlTqa" alt="" width="481"><figcaption><p>Additive motions</p></figcaption></figure>

IK Motions are applied similarly to recoil. Motions include aiming and fire‑mode transitions ("boop"/"jiggle" animations).

#### Leaning

Leaning distributes the angle evenly across spine bones:

<figure><img src="/files/v1WYGfMuVDBDCHusRNua" alt="" width="296"><figcaption><p>Leaning rotation.</p></figcaption></figure>

#### Curve-based animations

Movement (idle, walk, sprint, jumping) is applied directly to \*\*VB ik\\\_hand\\\_gun\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/N4leZavAN4rstKCQWzTa" alt="" width="512"><figcaption><p>Curve-based animations.</p></figcaption></figure>

Curve values (\*\*IK\\\_X/Y/Z\*\*, \*\*IK\\\_Pitch/Yaw/Roll\*\*) are \*\*retrieved\*\* here and plugged into the respective translation and rotation axes.

#### Aim Offset

Like leaning, \*\*Pitch\*\* is applied across spine bones to make the character look up and down:

<figure><img src="/files/SwBS4f8w6oiUB8IsnV8I" alt="" width="380"><figcaption><p>Spine bone rotation.</p></figcaption></figure>

#### IK and head bone

Finally, \*\*Two‑Bone IK\*\* nodes apply IK using \*\*VB hand\\\_r\*\* and \*\*VB hand\\\_l\*\* as targets. We also scale the head bone to zero if \`HideHeadMesh\` is \*\*true\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/LMordVn3gBTSr373CBYh" alt="" width="523"><figcaption><p>Final steps.</p></figcaption></figure>

---

# Procedural Recoil

## Recoil Animation

This Actor Component generates smooth weapon‑firing animation at runtime:

<figure><img src="/files/n05amF2FwwzDIYv5YLOX" alt="" width="385"><figcaption><p>Recoil Animation component.</p></figcaption></figure>

It is typically added to the character Blueprint, but you can also add it to the weapon. Call its methods as described below.

### Init

<figure><img src="/files/hjAQd8Oedt736SnxhlLg" alt="" width="366"><figcaption><p>Init method.</p></figcaption></figure>

Use this function to update active recoil settings. It requires a \*\*Recoil Data Asset\*\* and \*\*Fire Rate\*\* (rounds per minute).

### Play/Stop

<figure><img src="/files/r57by8rZF1tcstgMW9nE" alt="" width="372"><figcaption><p>Play, Stop functions.</p></figcaption></figure>

Call \*\*Play\*\* whenever a shot is fired, and \*\*Stop\*\* when the fire input is released.

### Set Aiming

Use this method to update the current aiming state:

<figure><img src="/files/bM1gOOb7r3yuzTkU5p35" alt="" width="374"><figcaption><p>Set Aiming.</p></figcaption></figure>

### Set Fire Mode

Use this method to update the current fire mode:

<figure><img src="/files/R8XegatYzm4UfaSCqRNb" alt="" width="427"><figcaption><p>Set Fire Mode.</p></figcaption></figure>

Three fire modes are supported:

\* Semi
\* Burst
\* Auto

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Note:\*\* For Recoil Animation, there is no technical difference between \*\*Burst\*\* and \*\*Auto\*\*.
{% endhint %}

## Recoil Animation Data

This Data Asset contains recoil properties, from simple inputs to complex layers such as \*\*Sway\*\* and \*\*Pushback\*\*.

### Input values

<figure><img src="/files/4bIZYxWIOWP09NW8j9pn" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Input Hip values.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*Hip\*\* and \*\*Aim\*\* properties define recoil ranges. For \*\*Yaw\*\* and \*\*Roll\*\*, a \`FVector4\` avoids values too close to zero. Final value is computed as:

{% code title="Pseudocode" overflow="wrap" %}

\`\`\`csharp
Yaw = Random(Random(YawHip.X, YawHip.Y), Random(YawHip.Z, YawHip.W));
\`\`\`

{% endcode %}

### Controller Recoil

Adds pitch and yaw to the controller rotation:

<figure><img src="/files/w0EPlwH6BBZCBQUnjrNf" alt="" width="433"><figcaption><p>Controller Recoil</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Horizontal Recoil Step\*\*: Value range for the yaw input.
\* \*\*Vertical Recoil Step\*\*: Value range for the pitch input.
\* \*\*Interp Speed\*\*: Recoil smoothing speed.
\* \*\*Damping\*\*: How fast recoil should recover.
\* \*\*Compensation\*\*: How much player input is used for compensation. \*\*1\*\* uses all input; \*\*0\*\* uses none (the weapon settles after firing).

### Recoil Noise

Random \*\*Y-Z\*\* (right/up) movement that adds translation noise when firing:

<figure><img src="/files/dbDrAnFRz63lXjcJosVe" alt="" width="411"><figcaption><p>Noise layer.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Target\*\*: Left and Up target values - these will be added every shot.
\* \*\*Interp Speed\*\*: Smoothing speed.
\* \*\*Damping\*\*: How fast noise should go to zero.
\* \*\*Aim Scalar\*\*: Multiplier applied when aiming.

### Pushback

This layer is applied on the second shot of a burst and auto sequence:

<figure><img src="/files/eCf1p9v85NHkJo4XC2LF" alt=""><figcaption><p><strong>Pushback</strong> layer.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Amount\*\*: How much backward translation to apply.
\* \*\*Damping\*\*: How fast the value should go to zero.
\* \*\*Interp Speed\*\*: General smoothing speed.

### Progress

Accumulates \*\*pitch\*\* and \*\*upward translation\*\* with every shot:

<figure><img src="/files/REIigAatVXCL8zh6aC8G" alt="" width="284"><figcaption><p>Progress layer.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Pitch Interp Speed\*\*: Smoothing pitch speed.
\* \*\*Pitch Damping\*\*: How fast pitch should go to zero.
\* \*\*Pitch Amount\*\*: How much pitch to add with each shot.
\* \*\*Up Interp Speed\*\*: Smoothing up speed.
\* \*\*Up Damping\*\*: How fast the up translation should go to zero.
\* \*\*Up Amount\*\*: How much up translation to add with each shot.
\* \*\*Ads Scale\*\*: Multiplier applied when aiming.

### Sway

\*\*Recoil Sway\*\* simulates arm offset when firing:

<figure><img src="/files/PHuYgjiELl6MOcGISviv" alt="" width="509"><figcaption><p>Sway properties.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Pitch Sway\*\*: A value from this range will be added to the pitch sway.
\* \*\*Yaw Sway\*\*: A value from this range will be added to the yaw sway.
\* \*\*Roll Sway Multiplier\*\*: Roll will be computed by multiplying the pitch sway with this value.
\* \*\*Damping\*\*: How fast the sway should go to zero.
\* \*\*Interp Speed\*\*: Sway smoothing speed.
\* \*\*Ads Scale\*\*: Multiplier applied when aiming.
\* \*\*Pivot Offset\*\*: Virtual pivot to add translation. Recommended \*\*X = 30-40\*\* units so the pivot is near the character’s shoulder.

### General

<figure><img src="/files/FLt3L3Y2oR7L2vNiOs1W" alt="" width="503"><figcaption><p>General recoil properties.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: Pivot Offsets apply recoil as if the weapon rotates around a point in world space. For example, with \`(-30, 0, 0)\`, recoil appears to pivot around the gun offset by that amount.
{% endhint %}

\* \*\*Space Rotation\*\*: Use only if animations have a different root orientation.
\* \*\*Play rate\*\*: Playback speed multiplier.

\*\*Playback Offset\*\* applies only to burst or auto fire modes. In these modes, recoil animation length equals the fire delay (\`60 / FireRate\`). \*\*Playback Offset\*\* adjusts that length to make the sequence longer or shorter.

### Curves

Recoil animation is curve‑driven. The solver uses \*\*Vector Curves\*\* for single and auto/burst fire modes:

<figure><img src="/files/dtks9MRskimzbbgKdxuk" alt="" width="367"><figcaption><p>Recoil curves.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: For single‑fire‑only weapons, set \*\*AutoRot\*\* and \*\*AutoLoc\*\* to \*\*None\*\*.How to get recoil values
{% endhint %}

To get recoil values, access the \`FTransform RecoilAnimation\` property:

<figure><img src="/files/JJ4FPHEa6B2PghlN1ER7" alt="" width="363"><figcaption><p>Recoil value.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: For example, plug this value into a \*\*Transform (Modify) Bone\*\* node in the Animation Blueprint.
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/PBxVfoa6nbUEOKeVVt7Q" alt="" width="533"><figcaption><p>How recoil is applied in the Tactial Shooter Pack.</p></figcaption></figure>

# Weapons

## BP\\\_TacticalShooterWeapon

This is the base weapon Blueprint class. It handles most player actions, including:

\* Draw/Holster
\* Firing
\* Inspecting
\* Mag Check
\* Reloading

### View Settings

<figure><img src="/files/yxOixFTu6btMfDteXIKt" alt="" width="332"><figcaption><p>UE4 and UE5 settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

Assign view settings for UE4 and UE5 mesh models here. When switching models, all weapons are forced to use the updated mannequin settings.

### Weapon Animations

<figure><img src="/files/NRhJHs2DTnaDfGgTK5Aa" alt="" width="354"><figcaption><p>Weapon Anim Montages.</p></figcaption></figure>

Assign all Anim Montages used by this weapon:

\* Draw
\* Holster
\* Reload Tactical
\* Reload Empty
\* Reload Start Empty
\* Reload Start Tactical
\* Reload Loop
\* Reload End
\* Inspect
\* Mag Check
\* Deploy Attachment (only used by Herrington 11-87 and SRM-12)
\* Stow Attachment (only used by Herrington 11-87 and SRM-12)
\* Fire
\* Fire Out

### Firing settings

<figure><img src="/files/eBEyJzv1aQ1NY6wLwZ6l" alt="" width="348"><figcaption><p>Recoil settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

Here you can assign the \[Procedural Recoil\](/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/procedural-recoil.md#recoil-animation-data) and \[First Person Camera\](/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/first-person-camera.md#camera-shakes) assets.

<figure><img src="/files/bfsnmzNER8HvqaLqnjsT" alt="" width="347"><figcaption><p>Weapon properties.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Max Ammo\*\*: Ammo capacity.
\* \*\*Fire Rate\*\*: Rounds per minute.
\* \*\*Burst Length\*\*: Number of rounds per burst.
\* \*\*Use Bursts\*\*: Enables burst mode.
\* \*\*Use Full Auto\*\*: Enables full‑auto.
\* \*\*Is One Handed\*\*: True for all pistols and \*\*sidearms\*\* (guns held with one hand).
\* \*\*Active Fire Mode\*\*: Default fire mode.

## BPI Tactical Shooter Weapon

This Blueprint Interface is used by Animation Blueprints to get essential weapon info.

<figure><img src="/files/6ePUu9agKWUbvVUL3iLG" alt="" width="170"><figcaption><p>Interface functions.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/RYcHnI4OyVXTQE3oPnX3" alt="" width="409"><figcaption><p>Returns current active fire mode.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/uWLdeRsxHKbVjiwk17M2" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Returns aim point FTransform.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*Aim Point\*\* is a socket transform relative to the weapon. In the example above, the default \`AimPoint\` socket is used; \*\*Component\*\* space means relative to the weapon.

<figure><img src="/files/AfH9VoCnOaCgVgprdh43" alt="" width="482"><figcaption><p>Resetting the weapon.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*Reload Weapon\*\* resets the current weapon ammo (e.g., when the reload animation ends).

<figure><img src="/files/VzRSovL0KcCo4sai99o0" alt="" width="408"><figcaption><p>Returns current and max ammo.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*Get Ammo\*\* returns current and max ammo (used to animate magazine parts: spring, follower, cartridges).

## Input blocking

While an action is playing (e.g., reload, mag check, inspect), weapons block other actions.

<figure><img src="/files/fnYbpAL4PIzhFEdFpy3c" alt="" width="289"><figcaption><p>Called when an action starts.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/QQvy9pbtsG7ujjW9DGXc" alt="" width="288"><figcaption><p>Called when an action ends.</p></figcaption></figure>

All actions have a check before starting an action:

<figure><img src="/files/TruAe42ssiumxJcLEds5" alt="" width="348"><figcaption><p>Only play actions if no action is playing.</p></figcaption></figure>

# Magazines

## Mag meshes

Magazine‑fed weapons (WK‑11 Viper, TR15, and SRM‑12) use separate Skeletal Meshes for magazines:

<figure><img src="/files/sBWXwwFclFMEEAFSoeli" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>TR15 base mesh.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/9cYNEo48eEKykrAm8EEJ" alt="" width="397"><figcaption><p>TR15 magazine mesh.</p></figcaption></figure>

Magazine skeletons each have their own Animation Blueprint, based on the \*\*ABP\\\_Magazine\*\* template.

## ABPs

This Blueprint samples the current magazine ammo to drive animation:

<figure><img src="/files/oXTPydyghF2zjddMCupn" alt="" width="557"><figcaption><p>How mags are animated.</p></figcaption></figure>

Weapon‑specific Blueprints override only these essential properties:

<figure><img src="/files/9Q7cc3ZvMtHBiEDwWZfa" alt="" width="305"><figcaption><p>Mag ABP properties.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Magazine Animation\*\*: Anim Sequence that animates the spring, follower, and cartridges.
\* \*\*Mag Capacity\*\*: Maximum ammo count for the magazine.
\* \*\*Anim Time Step\*\*: How much time (in seconds) it takes to process a single bullet.

# How to change character model

{% stepper %}
{% step %}

### Assign the skeleton

Right-click on your character mesh and click "Assign Skeleton". Next, pick the \*\*Manny\*\* skeleton from the Tactical Shooter Pack.

<figure><img src="/files/wkQO1IWoXQXbxa70eCaz" alt="" width="563"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Note\*\*: This will only work with models rigged to the UE5 Mannequin.
{% endhint %}
{% endstep %}

{% step %}

### Create a new Animation Blueprint

Pick the \*\*Manny\*\* skeleton from the Tactical Shooter Pack.

<figure><img src="/files/3u7vKOLSyYh6YMyZ6sR0" alt="" width="563"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

Open the blueprint, go to "Class Defaults" and set "Parent Class" to "ABP\\\_CopyPose".

<figure><img src="/files/XPQiXFKNmWwRnzleFxvt" alt="" width="563"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
{% endstep %}

{% step %}

### Add a new mesh component

Go to the \`BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter\` blueprint, add a new Skeletal Mesh Component and parent it to "Mesh".

<figure><img src="/files/ic8pH2Iveg2QRMF83vSz" alt="" width="494"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
{% endstep %}

{% step %}

### Assign the mesh and blueprint

Select the new mesh component and assign your custom character mesh and the animation blueprint.

<figure><img src="/files/2fvPRSbO3zCn2XPuXby5" alt="" width="496"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
{% endstep %}

{% step %}

### Adjust the main mesh

Finally, select the "Mesh" component and set its visibility flag to false.

<figure><img src="/files/9QWyBeNLNimYIpcIB5Wh" alt="" width="245"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

Search for "Anim Tick" and set it to "Always Tick Pose and Refresh Bones".

<figure><img src="/files/X8fZwsUM2wtDIRgHXzQs" alt="" width="496"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
{% endstep %}
{% endstepper %}

# Weapons

## Weapon ABPs

All guns have their own Animation Blueprint in \*\*/Animations/UE5/\*\*. Each has a simple structure consisting of a reference pose and a main \*\*Montage\*\* slot.

### WK-11 Viper ABP

<figure><img src="/files/KKHSDolcusTFEpk4BZwV" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>ABP\_WK-11\_Viper.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*DefaultSlot:\*\* Plays animations that animate the \*\*bolt slide\*\*.
\* \*\*MagSlot:\*\* Plays mag‑check and inspect animations to preserve the bolt‑slide state.

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: Bolt-slide state can change depending on the ammo state. For example, when out of ammo, the bolt will be locked back.
{% endhint %}

### TR15

<figure><img src="/files/yLS5NVYbp0OHgH0pwjRA" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>ABP\_TR15.</p></figcaption></figure>

Similar to the WK‑11 Viper, \*\*TR15\*\* also preserves its bolt state.

\* \*\*BoltSlot:\*\* Plays all animations that animate the bolt.
\* \*\*DefaultSlot:\*\* Used for all other animations.

### Herrington 11-87

<figure><img src="/files/SXWqQ047VowOrqxfk9a5" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>ABP\_Herrington\_11-87.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*DefaultSlot:\*\* Used for the inspect motion.
\* \*\*DefaultReload:\*\* Used for reload and firing motions.

### SRM-12

<figure><img src="/files/gwb4FdmqTQlNL74xzu24" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>ABP\_SRM-12.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*DefaultSlot:\*\* Used for animations that do not animate bipods.
\* \*\*BipodSlot:\*\* Used for bipod stow and deploy motions.

# First Person Camera

\*\*AC\\\_FirstPersonCamera\*\* is an Actor Component used to apply camera animations, recoil shakes, free look, and FOV updates at runtime.

## Initialization

<figure><img src="/files/JoFjCPLG21WaIIlM4sqB" alt="" width="239"><figcaption><p>Updating the target camera component.</p></figcaption></figure>

First, specify that the \*\*Camera\*\* will be animated. Use the default \`Camera\` component that comes with \`ACharacter\`.

Then, update the player mesh reference:

<figure><img src="/files/LEE38zUx5rSlmaWcR5Aj" alt="" width="479"><figcaption><p>Updating the character mesh.</p></figcaption></figure>

## Free Look

Free look is implemented by calling these methods on the Tactical Camera:

<figure><img src="/files/V1tvLniXNxDzSqDhgRPG" alt="" width="518"><figcaption><p>Handling free look.</p></figcaption></figure>

And by controlling player look input:

<figure><img src="/files/6Sm99eM6GcGeqXxNg2XC" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Taking free look input into account.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Note:\*\* During free look, only the camera should receive look input.
{% endhint %}

## FOV

The camera also updates the FOV when aiming:

<figure><img src="/files/LeLNObCno4P5ZV4EgAYB" alt="" width="368"><figcaption><p>Updating FOV.</p></figcaption></figure>

This logic switches between aiming and non‑aiming FOV states; the component handles FOV interpolation under the hood.

## Camera Shakes

The \`Play Camera Shake\` function is used to play shakes when firing:

<figure><img src="/files/tC4yGMoHUgb8crKCIgOU" alt="" width="425"><figcaption><p>How to play a camera shake.</p></figcaption></figure>

\`DA\_FirstPersonCameraShake\` is a Data Asset that contains shake settings:

<figure><img src="/files/Fcn5i2Ndl4SvHHHNA5Y3" alt="" width="384"><figcaption><p>Camera Shake settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Rotation Curve\*\*: Camera rotation animation.
\* \*\*Pitch\*\*/\*\*Yaw\*\*/\*\*Roll\*\*: Range of the pitch/yaw/roll value.
\* \*\*Smoothing\*\*: Interpolation speed.
\* \*\*Play Rate\*\*: Playback speed multiplier.

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/ik-motion-player.md).

# IK Motion Player

## Component

\*\*AC\\\_IKMotionPlayer\*\* generates procedural shakes when aiming or changing fire mode. It exposes a single method:

<figure><img src="/files/06y3MmqX0AVx9CwWYC3u" alt="" width="198"><figcaption><p>Use it to play a motion.</p></figcaption></figure>

To extract the animation \*\*values\*\* (translation & rotation), access the \`IKMotion\` property:

<figure><img src="/files/neaWEldymRzFoRIfxP2W" alt="" width="296"><figcaption><p>IK additive animation.</p></figcaption></figure>

## IK Motion

This Data Asset contains motion settings. To create a new IK Motion, right‑click and select \*\*Misc → Data Asset → IK Motion\*\*.

<figure><img src="/files/AMXr1FVu81FNj0FXHUEH" alt="" width="498"><figcaption><p>IK Motion data asset.</p></figcaption></figure>

\* \*\*Position\*\* and \*\*Rotation\*\*: Curves that define the animation for each translation and rotation axis.
\* \*\*Position/Rotation Scale:\*\* Controls the motion magnitude.
\* \*\*Blend Time\*\*: How long (in seconds) it takes to blend in a new motion.
\* \*\*Play Rate:\*\* Playback speed multiplier.

# Character | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/character.md)
.

How it's animated[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#how-its-animated)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

All animations target the **UE5 Mannequin** skeleton and rely on Epic’s IK bone set:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FteFCaPYTuQupNUA6lYG0%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db0272da6-699f-43ff-a7e8-ebb326dde088&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=db6bd5a5dc1a1db861a1fb7882d7b8c5&sv=3)

IK Bones

*   **ik\_hand\_gun** drives weapon movement..
    
*   **ik\_hand\_r** contains right‑hand animation data.
    
*   **ik\_hand\_l** contains left‑hand animation data.
    

The setup also uses **Virtual Bones (VBs)** for procedural animation:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FQRI7iIwfMZORammsXxmv%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7c554bab-8649-4039-8b7e-441d44a3b1bf&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b5e0e2741bb79aabeeaec061679e1044&sv=3)

Virtual Bone setup.

*   **VB ik\_hand\_gun** (targets **ik\_hand\_gun**): Used to position the pistol during quick draw so the **sidearm** is correctly placed relative to the **right hand**.
    
*   **VB ik\_hand\_gun\_pivot** (targets **ik\_hand\_gun**): All weapons are attached to this bone.
    
*   **VB hand\_r/l** (targets **hand\_r/l**): IK targets for the right/left hand.
    
*   **VB lowerarm\_r/l** (targets **lowerarm\_r/l**): Hint/pole targets for the right/left arm.
    
*   **VB recoil\_hand\_r** (targets **root**): Preserves right‑hand recoil when holding a pistol one‑handed.
    
*   **VB ik\_hand\_gun\_pose** (targets **root**): Used to compute the aiming pose.
    

**Tip**: Weapons are not parented to the right hand; they attach to **VB ik\_hand\_gun\_pivot**. This bone is blended in the Animation Blueprints so the final pose looks correct.

Animation Blueprints[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#animation-blueprints)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The pack uses modular **Animation Blueprints**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FmcgIcrW0su4fxF2D7wYm%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da095252d-91a6-4968-af98-c24ded538cff&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9d426988f30369914219e22e477e8ea5&sv=3)

Animation Blueprints used in the pack.

### ABP\_TacticalShooter\_UE5[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#abp_tacticalshooter_ue5)

This is the main Animation Blueprint; it incorporates the other animation assets. Here’s how it works:

#### Base character pose[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#base-character-pose)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FXnrP6nGOxoaIhZOmhwRm%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0014cd33-1f50-489b-ae00-30e7adb328a1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3d1d0e3790061cfc57fc210b92d814cf&sv=3)

Base pose.

The base combines a static idle **Anim Sequence** with the main **Anim Montage** (**DefaultSlot**).

#### Curve-based animations[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#curve-based-animations)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FOCVpGAIxteihqPng5HTU%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc076e6fc-2dcb-4248-8897-fad3c0b0a715&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1982a2e4f00b1bc5cedcbf258a656eca&sv=3)

Procedural movement graph.

General movement (idle, walk, sprint, jump) is curve‑driven and procedural, applied primarily via **Apply Additive** nodes.

The **Grounded** and **InAir** states provide animation curves such as:

*   **IK\_X**/**Y**/**Z**: position.
    
*   **IK\_Pitch**/**Yaw**/**Roll**: rotation.
    
*   **Camera Pitch**/**Yaw**/**Roll**: camera animation.
    

These curves are layered on top of the base pose. Finally, we blend **curve values** (not poses) using a helper bone:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FoSIRBVEn1M5hegrKwRDq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D952ccfcd-afb1-4eec-b9f0-ba28002456e6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=215011a257ec16810d2f7d6af1762302&sv=3)

Procedural movement blending.

**VB Curves** is a dummy bone used solely to blend curve values - not to animate a pose.

#### Pistol quick draw[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#pistol-quick-draw)

This layer applies quick‑draw and holster animations additively on top of the character pose:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FPLEbS1y6h7btJI1t63yt%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D265ace4c-42fc-4d27-81c4-28148ed5d1b1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=06d55b4f7768b666cca78585ae866b63&sv=3)

Pistol Quick Draw layer.

Computes an additive pose per weapon, letting a single animation work across different poses.

**Tip**: Originally authored for **TR15**, but reused across weapons via this layer.Left hand actions

Apply left‑hand actions (e.g., take an item, push a door):

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fgbpgz18dV2feZcPyxbKp%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D05059ab1-f1b7-43a7-bde0-b30e28be7722&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3637f91a11db9902720eedc1a6a53086&sv=3)

Left Hand Actions layer.

#### Finger firing motion[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#finger-firing-motion)

Animate the right index finger while firing:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FfVytG91Su08JLFkOOVLg%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D93f910f7-e272-4230-8b3a-669bb2943449&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=59929c9a810519120606b81297896341&sv=3)

Finger firing motion.

A float smoothly interpolates to **1** while firing to rotate the finger, then returns to **0** afterward.

### ABP\_IK\_Pose[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#abp_ik_pose)

Positions the weapon and hands before the main procedural pass. It works in stages:

#### VB ik\_hand\_gun\_r[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#vb-ik_hand_gun_r)

First, override **VB ik\_hand\_gun\_r** with the base idle pose when quick‑draw is enabled:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FtveIWOu52DCtPdFysVmr%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5906911d-8425-499e-855c-2a7fe7f6e08f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=079ef8579c8db8cb6bbf77bdb0c36e28&sv=3)

VB ik\_hand\_gun\_r override.

This is important for positioning the pistol properly.

#### Copying the animation data[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#copying-the-animation-data)

Copy the animation data from real bones to VBs. This lets us apply IK additively instead of **overriding** and breaking the pose:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F7NcgJMO1deugVmHvlCHX%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D323cf87a-49fe-4cdd-90aa-f5327eb29580&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3e308b24882516203910dcd267e39009&sv=3)

Copying the animation data.

Then override **VB hand\_r** and **VB hand\_l** with the current grip pose:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F4OPAgw3wK61k1X36qvM9%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9005c312-0e59-4ef4-aa85-11257100dda7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6135d584f346dbbc36062d0eb91b90db&sv=3)

Left hand pose override.

By default, all weapons attach the left hand to the gun. You can smoothly disable this via a curve - for example, many animations **do this** to let the left hand move freely:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FbCEUaL8mUAfpvMYFlw0U%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df0e3322c-7cb9-475b-91ae-e1002dd7c9aa&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ece127df684cb3d191becf890112ea41&sv=3)

**Mask\_Grip** can disable the left‑hand attachment.

#### Offsets[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#offsets)

Finally, apply **FTransform** offsets to all IK bones.

### ABP\_IK\_Ads[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#abp_ik_ads)

Applies procedural aiming in two steps:

1.  Snap the weapon to the camera socket.
    
2.  Apply the **Aim Point** local transform.
    

Note: The **FPCamera** socket is attached to **neck\_02**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F5lcTv1csf35Ao0EcnKez%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da8f2512c-8356-420b-8059-127042b5e1f2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=adcd6995ebd6e37684f82814ecb51918&sv=3)

Camera socket.

### ABP\_IK\_Sway[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#abp_ik_sway)

Applies aiming and movement sway to **VB ik\_hand\_gun**. Sway is computed in **AC\_TacticalShooterAnimation**; this layer simply **retrieves** the **FTransform** and applies it to the bone:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FWIbsxkZaACOwbfc7R4hp%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8a4af3e6-814a-4e21-9531-c2ad0b999715&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ac7f4e097b9448616715de0596be174c&sv=3)

Sway layer.

### ABP\_IK\_Main[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#abp_ik_main)

The final Animation Blueprint, responsible for:

1.  Recoil
    
2.  IK Motions
    
3.  Leaning and Aim Offset
    
4.  IK
    
5.  Hiding the head bone
    

#### Recoil[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#recoil)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FmAzRV3NZfD6qz7crVBpl%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D302ecf71-fc85-4852-8c71-46f4349cced0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6794eef1d6e609e108c04ffc6bd61ab6&sv=3)

How recoil is applied.

Recoil animation values are **retrieved** from the **Recoil Animation** component and applied via **Transform (Modify) Bone**. We preserve the right‑hand pose before applying recoil (needed for pistol quick draw).

#### IK Motions[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#ik-motions)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FemltrqMCE7gUd1SDWzoU%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da88ffa44-6b7c-46fe-86b0-86f1cdd33d57&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a64c7d802e9db96fc3b5d84a44463ef7&sv=3)

Additive motions

IK Motions are applied similarly to recoil. Motions include aiming and fire‑mode transitions ("boop"/"jiggle" animations).

#### Leaning[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#leaning)

Leaning distributes the angle evenly across spine bones:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fn3tZISYjGXhMOrOTW6ep%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De6cd8f26-cd3c-4267-8fb3-43e23df2dda5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5e3f3ec4fe006edab816aac6b2421edc&sv=3)

Leaning rotation.

#### Curve-based animations[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#curve-based-animations-1)

Movement (idle, walk, sprint, jumping) is applied directly to **VB ik\_hand\_gun**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FWEOaugYOs7UTZEbJ5yqp%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8edb89ed-b65d-419e-85dd-ffa0bd416db2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=df2b380552de138abd632005c742ba96&sv=3)

Curve-based animations.

Curve values (**IK\_X/Y/Z**, **IK\_Pitch/Yaw/Roll**) are **retrieved** here and plugged into the respective translation and rotation axes.

#### Aim Offset[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#aim-offset)

Like leaning, **Pitch** is applied across spine bones to make the character look up and down:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F6AIv3qvYBu4adEoCKbkS%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D61d8e352-52c9-4637-a9b4-38be1d8ff901&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=20baac32647a39fdf1a4b2c238d68e2c&sv=3)

Spine bone rotation.

#### IK and head bone[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations#ik-and-head-bone)

Finally, **Two‑Bone IK** nodes apply IK using **VB hand\_r** and **VB hand\_l** as targets. We also scale the head bone to zero if `HideHeadMesh` is **true**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Ftw9KKtj5ac0UtK3IvAhn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddf2b098b-40ec-493b-9e5f-3bec7c5af3ce&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3b9a53e6777fddd47a1e344e5670691f&sv=3)

Final steps.

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/tactical-shooter-animation.md).

# Tactical Shooter Animation

\*\*AC\\\_TacticalShooterAnimation\*\* is a central Actor Component that serves as an interface between gameplay and animation. It stores and updates weapon settings and input values (e.g., leaning angle, current gait).

## Tactical View Settings

This data asset is covered in detail here: \[Procedural Animation Settings\](/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/procedural-animation-settings.md). To update the current settings, it's necessary to directly update the reference:

<figure><img src="/files/bfNrpL7lxvoZHIAzzeeu" alt="" width="488"><figcaption><p>Updating settings when equipping a new weapon.</p></figcaption></figure>

## Inputs

This component also \*\*stores\*\* player input information, specifically:

### Leaning Input

Controls how much the player can rotate left and right to \*\*peek\*\* around corners:

<figure><img src="/files/wAtw7upJj0BQt1Pi1A5d" alt="" width="441"><figcaption><p>Updating leaning angle.</p></figcaption></figure>

### Gait

Represents the current movement state in the range \*\*\\\[0–2\]\*\*, where:

\* 0 stands for idle
\* 1 stands for walk
\* 2 stands for sprint

Used to control the blending of idle, walk, and sprint animations:

<figure><img src="/files/TqcgvnUT4D4bOVWB4ljg" alt="" width="276"><figcaption><p>Updating movement gait.</p></figcaption></figure>

### Is Aiming

Used to control procedural aiming:

<figure><img src="/files/28rJhMcXyk2YgPUu7R1H" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Updating aiming.</p></figcaption></figure>

### Is Firing

This flag has two applications in Animation Blueprints:

1. Rotating the right index finger to press the trigger.
2. Controlling the idle sway intensity when firing.

<figure><img src="/files/aJ52q2YnTt7H96hCeRim" alt="" width="354"><figcaption><p>Whether firing is active or not.</p></figcaption></figure>

# Sounds

All action sounds are played using the \*\*Play Sound\*\* notify attached to weapon animations:

<figure><img src="/files/wNQg6U6giP2I82bAiSnA" alt="" width="320"><figcaption><p>Play Sound notify.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/o3vWzuMaaG1mKOjkXOTF" alt="" width="329"><figcaption><p>Sound settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

The only exception is firing sounds, which are played via the weapon Blueprint:

<figure><img src="/files/x1PmX164Gblr6PXzeH9o" alt="" width="289"><figcaption><p>Firing sounds.</p></figcaption></figure>

# Player | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/player.md)
.

Overview[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints#overview)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter** is a game‑ready player Blueprint responsible for managing weapons, inputs, and actions:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FIXA7np59yB2VCQlyoaXK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dae4e0207-2e6f-471a-8892-db337f861ba1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=85847b5a57c68b4735c2b3520dfa3578&sv=3)

Tactical Shooter Character.

[🔷First Person Camera](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/first-person-camera)
[🔷Tactical Shooter Animation](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/tactical-shooter-animation)
[🔷IK Motion Player](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints/ik-motion-player)

Character Settings[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints#character-settings)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**TacticalCharacterSettings** is a Data Asset that tells the player Blueprint which meshes, Animation Blueprints, and weapons to use:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F2j5lvCP1eVWXFD785SBG%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3a3e8bf0-c899-4ecb-9a3f-e807491f25ae&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3b58183593c7dc0e30d85492448a7afd&sv=3)

Character Settings.

The pack supports switching between UE4 and UE5 meshes at runtime, so you can specify different skeletons and Animation Blueprints. By default, the UE5 mesh is used.

The **Weapons** list contains weapon Blueprint classes used by the character.

BPI Tactical Shooter Character[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints#bpi-tactical-shooter-character)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This Blueprint Interface connects the player with animation systems and third‑party systems:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FdGxpWuJtRLCKxDmjhkPn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8024b36b-81b3-49c4-b8c6-576ab837b916&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5464ed1a689dbc2f7d272806237b5262&sv=3)

Interface functions.

*   **Get Player Mesh:** Returns the player's **Skeletal Mesh Component**.
    
*   **Get Primary Weapon:** Returns the currently equipped weapon. When quick‑drawing a **sidearm**, it returns the pistol.
    
*   **Get Main Weapon:** Returns the main weapon.
    
*   **Get Tactical Shooter Component:** Returns the `AC_TacticalShooterAnimation` component.
    

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FnBlm7SPaxCztlxMC4XFc%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Debe94dc9-d721-448b-be70-082333613a60&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=87895b3f1ecec321af8f22f18508870d&sv=3)

Returns the player mesh here.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FkLxdhhit3Kv6k6b0Q2TQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D19c00b97-4dc5-4ece-a618-843c869f1332&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ac368cdd86485d3e9e3aefe278388a31&sv=3)

Returns the currently used weapon.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fw4wCxdqSwqNQNfG53qbu%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc8823f93-4f9e-4b6b-b319-2dc02973b88b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7d748874f9a453a0060bd7859696f096&sv=3)

Returns the current weapon.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FoAeXoRj1WTYdLppiHsjY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1a5828c4-00d7-484f-9bf8-93faf95e2780&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8b7cc9c361647c3370be08c7a75009f3&sv=3)

Returns a reference to the animation component.

Input Actions[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/blueprints#input-actions)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

All Input Actions are implemented in this Blueprint. Here is the list of actions used in the pack:

*   **Weapon-specific**
    
    *   Reload
        
    *   Mag Check
        
    *   Inspect
        
    *   Fire
        
    
*   **General**
    
    *   Aiming
        
    *   Change Weapon
        
    *   Change Fire Mode
        
    *   Look
        
    *   Move
        
    *   Sprint
        
    *   Free Look
        
    *   Pistol Quick Draw
        
    *   Lean

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/animations/procedural-animation-settings.md).

# Procedural Animation Settings

## Tactical Shooter View Settings

Data Asset that contains animation properties used by gameplay and \*\*Animation Blueprints\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/DINXksSN1KjwUNvOmBfb" alt="" width="458"><figcaption><p>Tactical Shooter View settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

### General

\* \*\*Idle Pose\*\*: Base idle pose for the weapon.
\* \*\*Character Animations\*\*: Struct listing all \*\*Anim Montages\*\* used by the Blueprints.

### Procedural

\* \*\*Viewmodel\*\*: Contains offsets for first‑person view.
  \* \*\*Pivot Offset\*\*: Weapon position offset.
  \* \*\*Pose Offset\*\*: FTransform offset for weapon and arms.
  \* \*\*Sprint Offset\*\*: FTransform offset, applied only when sprinting.
  \* \*\*Right\*\*/\*\*Left Hand Offset\*\*: Position offset for the right/left hand.
  \* LeftElbowSprintOffset: Left elbow offset applied only when sprinting.
\* \*\*Pistol Quick Draw Viewmodel\*\*: Has the same exact properties as the Viewmodel.
\* \*\*Aiming\*\*/\*\*Movement Sway\*\*
  \* \*\*Stiffness\*\*: Oscillation factor in range \\\[0;1\].
  \* \*\*Damping\*\*: Critical damping factor in range \\\[0;1\].
  \* \*\*Interp Speed\*\*: Smoothing speed.
  \* \*\*Smoothing Speed\*\*: How fast the value should go to zero.
  \* \*\*Clamp Value\*\*: Max allowed value.

## Locomotion

\* \*\*Walk\*\* and \*\*Sprint Intensity\*\*: Controls the influence of walk and sprint animations.

## How these assets are used

<figure><img src="/files/fJRIwa7o7clFBA4nrKvY" alt=""><figcaption><p>All View Settings used in the package.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*Tactical Shooter View Settings\*\* are used in both gameplay and \*\*Animation Blueprints\*\*. Each weapon has two settings assets: one for \*\*UE5\*\* animations and one for \*\*UE4\*\* animations.

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: The Tactical Shooter Pack allows switching \*\*UE4\*\* and \*\*UE5\*\* meshes at runtime.
{% endhint %}
