# GASP 5.7+ | Tactical Shooter Pack Unreal

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+.md)
.

Project Download (UE 5.7 only)[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#project-download-ue-5.7-only)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Follow these steps to download and install the **Unreal Engine 5.7** integration project.

1

### Download the project[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#download-the-project)

[![Logo](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fssl.gstatic.com%2Fdocs%2Fdoclist%2Fimages%2Fdrive_favicon_2026_32dp.png&width=20&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=23d7dd57b363375d08c2dedd7e92cf00&sv=3)TacticalShooterPack\_Integration\_GASP.zipGoogle Docs](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1h5zzf56JRX-7mo1XtNu_U3djJSk5S6pk/view?usp=drive_link)

2

### Import the Tactical Shooter Pack[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#import-the-tactical-shooter-pack)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FiX1BcBoHtI5H8d70CpVN%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6bf4251f-52f1-4057-806c-febc5fd690cb&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f8259c646fc734009dc4c0d5544d7706&sv=3)

Via Fab Launcher.

Select the project you just downloaded, then add the pack content to it.

**Tip**: For manual installation, copy the “KINEMATION” folder from the Tactical Shooter Pack download and paste it into the downloaded integration package.

Character Meshes[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#character-meshes)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#### Base Blueprint[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#base-blueprint)

First, we need to create a base for our integration. Duplicate the **BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter** (**KINEMATION/TacticalShooterPack/Blueprints**):

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FEab8rXK8OhDXL54KTvxw%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc714e553-ca9d-4ad0-9451-db77fc2c0d1b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0e99b49b5f7a136e1b23f3be5b337a23&sv=3)

New blueprint.

This will be our main player blueprint. Open it up, go to **Class Settings** and inherit it from the **SandboxCharacter\_CMC**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FkkEZxEFpvCfDpTVwYOhy%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc6f9694c-55b2-47aa-b961-cb78ed7f02ef&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0670fc8d3db33213ef3b8371b11f8bbf&sv=3)

Change the parent class.

Now we need to add a skeletal mesh for our character model. Let's call it "Player":

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F5empN3aC8LMmjiGRZP0V%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8ab63f9e-c6ab-4211-88fd-1cfb1f179829&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ceff3ca0a4f2a799bd4f0be91776272f&sv=3)

Add this new Skeletal Mesh Component.

Next, assign the modular skeletal mesh — we'll use our modular Operator model:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FsdiyyJOLDNfPjQktZqzt%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D435a782f-9163-4cb6-8f0e-73cd08a218ad&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=82416c263ff60f73ecb41203ee74ba14&sv=3)

Pick this skeletal mesh.

Find the `GetPlayerMesh` interface function and return the new "Player" skeletal mesh component:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F1onRx0FGiMkoUHv3eSMN%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4e428402-9353-4ff7-895e-23a79806e706&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f85ade8e7861720d148ba3716da62074&sv=3)

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F2qz9YPmFycEyCkAJUJFQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7255936d-547c-4139-91ef-4ed99bd4ec76&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c56f617572bf55a66835c873a38705e7&sv=3)

#### Motion Matching Mesh[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#motion-matching-mesh)

Select the "Mesh" component and set its animation blueprint to **SandboxCharacter\_CMC\_ABP** and skeletal mesh to **SKM\_UEFN\_Mannequin**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FvF53S3lNhmlFRZgE2bdW%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfa797307-8979-49f1-905c-9bfbcdc074db&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8af9de2889ef0ff22ac7758acfff7848&sv=3)

Select the Mesh component in the hierarchy.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FSikpv3mB4dKSFkkGVsO3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D26a0ed8d-e141-43ee-a9ce-2e62b36cc61f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d21c74e4dd6d3f8c8f7a72a82d1e2b1f&sv=3)

Update the Anim Class and Skeletal Mesh Asset.

**Note**: The "Mesh" skeletal component will generate a motion-matching pose (full-body movement), which will be blended with our tactical animations.

#### First Person Camera[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#first-person-camera)

Parent the **Camera** component to the Player mesh we created earlier:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FGUENlywacuJ1Tb9SntAW%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D484fb880-06cc-4d46-ae68-b2003c9ac663&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=88d001bcd61606ce34915e81eed72bf3&sv=3)

Select the **FPCamera** as a target attachment socket and reset its position and rotation values:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FvE3UPE8L9WPGqnQgojDe%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0cb2cb09-9da0-491a-84b6-19f09d06ae2d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=178b8eb27d4b3a06efc2535e813baaa8&sv=3)

#### Disabling GASP Cameras & Mesh[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#disabling-gasp-cameras-and-mesh)

To disable GASP camera logic and hide the default mesh, we need to add a couple of code snippets at the end of the Begin Play event.

To hide the default **Sandbox** mesh, add these nodes to the graph:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FWBqC378zmit53U38huOT%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0b0a2fee-4206-41d4-aaa8-c1c4397f720a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5474c766159ff9b7d7b1b098b010cb36&sv=3)

How to hide the base mesh.

To disable the default camera logic, use this code snippet

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FlFofXOOMNCO0iv9iWz7G%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc9920bd1-afc0-4b7f-ba9e-c57788705def&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ce24693ea051d33188ea1d61a13272e7&sv=3)

Add this right after Begin Play.

Here's a video showing where to put this logic in the player blueprint:

Inputs[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#inputs)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The next step is to properly update GASP inputs at runtime. Open the `Update Player Movement` function (it's called on **Tick**):

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FWUbmsp1zl6dpydDaVtsk%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deaa8b0c9-df35-4253-a837-d72aa411eb0e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e229d844773e3d031a185d6f0d221a97&sv=3)

Update movement function.

Find some place at the end of the function, and set the value of the Gait property based on Movement State:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FQQYXOyetTFwkXY9xvGo3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8bc23a45-8d27-450e-a1df-aeb3b365fae7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d6cfa393b5a7419103de5914332c3f3e&sv=3)

Updating current Gait.

**Note**: `Gait` is from **GASP**, and `Movement State` is a **Tactical Shooter Pack** property.

Then, we need to update `bWantsToSprint`, `bWantsToWalk`, `bWantsToAim` and `bWantsToStrafe` properties.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FXNJcaEAl7u7QZVg4QQkn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D28828ddc-609a-40e9-a97a-5ea6db1d67d1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fbf38b3dd8db49085758218ab3d6883d&sv=3)

Animation Blueprint[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#animation-blueprint)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Now we need to create an animation blueprint (ABP) that will combine motion-matching pose and animations from the **Tactical Shooter Pack**.

Create a new Animation Blueprint using the **UE5 Manny** skeleton from the pack:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FbYPfPtihznedbGRK4oq1%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1cc08a3-afc6-429b-aa66-1dc940df0aa5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e7e1b312dfc6e1d56c08c34e89fe4e91&sv=3)

Creating a new animation blueprint.

Go to the Event Graph and use this snippet for initialization:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FOhBRSilQwqqLwJg9wAnq%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0f78eac5-1836-4339-8731-b5672ecdf1b2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4483b7c30383558fa3388483d68bb565&sv=3)

Simple initialization logic.

**Tip**_:_ Obtain a reference to your player Blueprint here.

In the **Anim Graph**, add a **Retarget Pose From Mesh** node. Set the `Retarget From` to **Custom Skeletal Mesh Component**, and `IK Retargeter Asset` to **RTG\_UEFN\_to\_UE5\_Mannequin**:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252Fiu4clvunWjSQStc0XinS%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D82b29459-c14a-483d-b208-5fb41277dbc7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ed5c23c65f2b671e134ddbab8e96e734&sv=3)

Retargeting settings.

After that, bind the `Source Mesh Component` using the "Mesh" property from the player blueprint.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FgC9FZjqvIC0XdFwiNf8Q%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8b3ca6fe-4583-4a1d-b0a7-68e7a2ed7c7d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d6dfe6e2f3ae0e7b8ddbc24032e6b270&sv=3)

Bind the "Mesh" here.

Next, add the ABP\_TacticalShooter\_UE5 linked anim instance and blend it with the retargeted pose:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FECVoSVrc5T1ewF6R9fi7%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D55a4796e-00cc-4db8-8ed5-52a001718858&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c789fc16f03b0348ce087c3447a60df3&sv=3)

Simple blending setup.

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FxGGlTX4W3LB6MbTvsJZR%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D145b5ed9-3543-48f6-9a4f-aa46b1c81caa&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1021ba0a342656ecc3b9af4443632b12&sv=3)

Override the upper body.

**Tip**: Feed the retargeted pose into **Base Pose**, and Tactical Shooter animations into **Blend Pose 0**.

Parkour adjustments[](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/tutorial/gasp-integration/gasp-5.7+#parkour-adjustments)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy the jumping logic from `SandboxCharacter_CMC`:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FMiWLLwFavwsNlmfo5TI5%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7755aaa0-b8d9-420b-8e8a-3f167f1df2f8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=71810f1e11fadc800b52c6e99ba06c41&sv=3)

Copy this logic to "BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter\_GASP".

Next, we need to adjust our layering in the animation blueprint. First, create 2 cached poses:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FhJUGVmudxeK418M7f4AK%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D64e7ba04-0121-4c16-a462-4150d088f6bf&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=80ff680f7cb413dd7573b78f4992e60a&sv=3)

Cached poses.

**UpperBody** carries Tactical Shooter animations; **MotionMatchingPose** carries full‑body motion.

Next, add a new **Layered Blend Per Bone** node:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FXoWy5AJlXPxhVBVF77Do%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7119e17a-40b7-42f4-88ee-a5452b306c0f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5c482ebfd265abdd5f236a317819e930&sv=3)

This pose will be used when climbing.

Add a **Blend by Bool** node and plug the layered result into **True** link:

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252F7JBnucvb75WwLF9IMxRc%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcc34a872-f503-40a9-908c-77b55897b519&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c622e679ffae568a50c8931c3ae5d917&sv=3)

Use the "Upper Body" as a "False Pose".

Right-click on the "Active Value" and bind "**BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter\_GASP▸AC\_TraversalLogic ▸ DoingTraversalAction**".

Finally, blend the result with the "MotionMatchingPose":

![](https://kinemation.gitbook.io/tactical-shooter-pack-unreal/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3708064705-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fi3pgkdnEMLV7oBpvsWiN%252Fuploads%252FhRVsxJoyE20zW1PzFijH%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df494d262-1cb6-4834-b144-19f02947fe96&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=98063b7e9398e3895618d669ea85ebb2&sv=3)

Final blending step.

# GASP 5.7+

## Project Download (UE 5.7 only)

Follow these steps to download and install the \*\*Unreal Engine 5.7\*\* integration project.

{% stepper %}
{% step %}

### Download the project

{% embed url="<https://drive.google.com/file/d/1h5zzf56JRX-7mo1XtNu\_U3djJSk5S6pk/view?usp=drive\_link>" %}
{% endstep %}

{% step %}

### Import the Tactical Shooter Pack

<figure><img src="/files/Ky6byhkvdoHG05dgKW89" alt=""><figcaption><p>Via Fab Launcher.</p></figcaption></figure>

Select the project you just downloaded, then add the pack content to it.

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: For manual installation, copy the “KINEMATION” folder from the Tactical Shooter Pack download and paste it into the downloaded integration package.
{% endhint %}
{% endstep %}
{% endstepper %}

## Character Meshes

#### Base Blueprint

First, we need to create a base for our integration. Duplicate the \*\*BP\\\_TacticalShooterCharacter\*\* (<mark style="background-color:purple;">\*\*KINEMATION/TacticalShooterPack/Blueprints\*\*</mark>):

<figure><img src="/files/11pgqsetFPfBryn1jazM" alt="" width="102"><figcaption><p>New blueprint.</p></figcaption></figure>

This will be our main player blueprint. Open it up, go to \*\*Class Settings\*\* and inherit it from the \*\*SandboxCharacter\\\_CMC\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/m7sLQMNWm9ONAEk1crdM" alt=""><figcaption><p>Change the parent class.</p></figcaption></figure>

Now we need to add a skeletal mesh for our character model. Let's call it "Player":

<figure><img src="/files/TxToBQv3GCF9pvIy5s9h" alt="" width="362"><figcaption><p>Add this new Skeletal Mesh Component.</p></figcaption></figure>

Next, assign the modular skeletal mesh — we'll use our modular Operator model:

<figure><img src="/files/e0lClGr9XC8g83GSZur6" alt="" width="546"><figcaption><p>Pick this skeletal mesh.</p></figcaption></figure>

Find the \`GetPlayerMesh\` interface function and return the new "Player" skeletal mesh component:

<figure><img src="/files/BxbVl9xYzt9xJKbhqOgP" alt="" width="277"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/k9dVqdSHlLRJ7HmVoh2T" alt="" width="392"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

#### Motion Matching Mesh

Select the "Mesh" component and set its animation blueprint to \*\*SandboxCharacter\\\_CMC\\\_ABP\*\* and skeletal mesh to \*\*SKM\\\_UEFN\\\_Mannequin\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/jfk1f32v7rC2kVmGHOS2" alt="" width="376"><figcaption><p>Select the Mesh component in the hierarchy.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/VpzrtV5yXbQDjZxj5ghj" alt="" width="477"><figcaption><p>Update the Anim Class and Skeletal Mesh Asset.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Note\*\*: The "Mesh" skeletal component will generate a motion-matching pose (full-body movement), which will be blended with our tactical animations.
{% endhint %}

#### First Person Camera

Parent the \*\*Camera\*\* component to the Player mesh we created earlier:

<figure><img src="/files/B7HtOzGja1kk0yL6dImd" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

Select the \*\*FPCamera\*\* as a target attachment socket and reset its position and rotation values:

<figure><img src="/files/G5Qr3wmd2IykH85fnO8l" alt="" width="434"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

#### Disabling GASP Cameras & Mesh

To disable GASP camera logic and hide the default mesh, we need to add a couple of code snippets at the end of the Begin Play event.

To hide the default \*\*Sandbox\*\* mesh, add these nodes to the graph:

<figure><img src="/files/hYWybTmcHx0QILaf6TWp" alt=""><figcaption><p>How to hide the base mesh.</p></figcaption></figure>

To disable the default camera logic, use this code snippet

<figure><img src="/files/maQY3hQk4zFMUnKhvbwS" alt=""><figcaption><p>Add this right after Begin Play.</p></figcaption></figure>

Here's a video showing where to put this logic in the player blueprint:

{% embed url="<https://drive.google.com/file/d/1khr\_9oOfYj-OBmOJF\_gT5b0esx9HLj1j/view?usp=sharing>" %}

## Inputs

The next step is to properly update GASP inputs at runtime. Open the \`Update Player Movement\` function (it's called on \*\*Tick\*\*):

<figure><img src="/files/1ddSCH7gnVcBfIW5kOBO" alt="" width="483"><figcaption><p>Update movement function.</p></figcaption></figure>

Find some place at the end of the function, and set the value of the Gait property based on Movement State:

<figure><img src="/files/53QoiphVkf5wvW2w7Y4D" alt="" width="377"><figcaption><p>Updating current Gait.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Note\*\*: \`Gait\` is from \*\*GASP\*\*, and \`Movement State\` is a \*\*Tactical Shooter Pack\*\* property.
{% endhint %}

Then, we need to update \`bWantsToSprint\`, \`bWantsToWalk\`, \`bWantsToAim\` and \`bWantsToStrafe\` properties.

<figure><img src="/files/a8q5vmu2KGNyoRO55qvu" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## Animation Blueprint

Now we need to create an animation blueprint (ABP) that will combine motion-matching pose and animations from the \*\*Tactical Shooter Pack\*\*.

Create a new Animation Blueprint using the \*\*UE5 Manny\*\* skeleton from the pack:

<figure><img src="/files/kaEZTKbKXlE1uTj3ttdU" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Creating a new animation blueprint.</p></figcaption></figure>

Go to the Event Graph and use this snippet for initialization:

<figure><img src="/files/4rP0UelaBgKZdoibuDe8" alt=""><figcaption><p>Simple initialization logic.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*\*:\* Obtain a reference to your player Blueprint here.
{% endhint %}

In the \*\*Anim Graph\*\*, add a \*\*Retarget Pose From Mesh\*\* node. Set the \`Retarget From\` to \*\*Custom Skeletal Mesh Component\*\*, and \`IK Retargeter Asset\` to \*\*RTG\\\_UEFN\\\_to\\\_UE5\\\_Mannequin\*\*:

<figure><img src="/files/EMcoXAu4jyNkWe4BCH7Q" alt="" width="556"><figcaption><p>Retargeting settings.</p></figcaption></figure>

After that, bind the \`Source Mesh Component\` using the "Mesh" property from the player blueprint.

<figure><img src="/files/VBbzJ4h8HvARthEdFQ2i" alt="" width="265"><figcaption><p>Bind the "Mesh" here.</p></figcaption></figure>

Next, add the ABP\\\_TacticalShooter\\\_UE5 linked anim instance and blend it with the retargeted pose:

<figure><img src="/files/WmmOtJn7k1i1uQH6xdhH" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Simple blending setup.</p></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/xg9W9vbZW1tTJZSYgLv3" alt="" width="563"><figcaption><p>Override the upper body.</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %}
\*\*Tip\*\*: Feed the retargeted pose into \*\*Base Pose\*\*, and Tactical Shooter animations into \*\*Blend Pose 0\*\*.
{% endhint %}

## Parkour adjustments

Copy the jumping logic from \`SandboxCharacter\_CMC\`:

<figure><img src="/files/TX80OSjwRf59HdtUaGEx" alt=""><figcaption><p>Copy this logic to "BP\_TacticalShooterCharacter\_GASP".</p></figcaption></figure>

Next, we need to adjust our layering in the animation blueprint. First, create 2 cached poses:

<figure><img src="/files/H7QoC3bNEHFk0uGlYZp6" alt="" width="449"><figcaption><p>Cached poses.</p></figcaption></figure>

\*\*UpperBody\*\* carries Tactical Shooter animations; \*\*MotionMatchingPose\*\* carries full‑body motion.

Next, add a new \*\*Layered Blend Per Bone\*\* node:

<figure><img src="/files/vIofnoH1qhZa3gdpQRUk" alt=""><figcaption><p>This pose will be used when climbing.</p></figcaption></figure>

Add a \*\*Blend by Bool\*\* node and plug the layered result into \*\*True\*\* link:

<figure><img src="/files/myKsp3uVzPTdmLOa1YsH" alt="" width="524"><figcaption><p>Use the "Upper Body" as a "False Pose".</p></figcaption></figure>

Right-click on the "Active Value" and bind "\*\*BP\\\_TacticalShooterCharacter\\\_GASP▸AC\\\_TraversalLogic ▸ DoingTraversalAction\*\*".

Finally, blend the result with the "MotionMatchingPose":

<figure><img src="/files/YYNN3IudvpYPyBVRq81q" alt=""><figcaption><p>Final blending step.</p></figcaption></figure>

---
